Power Charges can be gained through three methods: APPARATUS SIZE Apparatus Size Wear Ride Small 1 Small or larger creature 1 Medium or smaller creature Medium 1 Medium or larger creature 1 Medium creature or 2 Small or smaller creatures Large 1 Large or larger creature 2 Medium or smaller creatures Huge 1 Huge or larger creature 1 Large creature or 4 Medium or smaller creatures Gargantuan 1 Gargantuan creature 1 Huge creature, 2 Large creatures, or 6 Medium or smaller creatures

• Natural Power. During a Long Rest, you power up your Apparatus using the natural world around you such as wind, water, or thermal energy, or simply through handcranks or exercise equipment. After finishing a Long Rest, the Apparatus gains a number of Power Charges up to your Charge Capacity. • Energy Cells. You can insert an energy cell into your Apparatus as an action or Bonus Action, which causes it to gain a number of Power Charges up to your Charge Capacity. You can swap out a spent energy cell for a new one as an action or Bonus Action as well. • Charge Restoration. If an effect would restore charges to a nonmagical object and targets your Apparatus, it gains an equal number of Power Charges instead.

### Level 2: Equipment Expert

When you use an object from the Adventuring Gear section (either in Fifth Edition gaming rules or chapter 3 of this book) that targets only one creature, object, or space, such as Alchemist’s Fire or a vial of Acid, you can target a second creature, object, or space within 10 feet of the target with the effect of the same object. In addition, you can add your Intelligence modifier to the damage you deal or Hit Points you restore with such objects (minimum of +1).

### Level 2: Signature Functions

When you gain this feature, choose up to a number of Functions you know equal to your Proficiency Bonus as Signature Functions. While your Apparatus is broken, while within 5 feet of it you can pick up one of these Signature Functions as if you were drawing a weapon and use it. These Signature Functions use the same Power Charges as your Apparatus. If a Signature Function states its effect applies to your Apparatus, it instead applies to you. If a Signature Function requires an item, you must have the item on your person. When your Apparatus is repaired, these Signature Functions immediately reassemble with it and are inaccessible for your use until the Apparatus breaks apart again. At the end of a Short Rest, you can replace one of your Signature Functions with a different Function you know.

### Level 3: Mechanist Subclass

You gain a Mechanist subclass of your choice. The Builder, Engineer, and Magi-Technician subclasses are detailed after this class’ description. A subclass is a specialization that grants you features at certain Mechanist levels. For the rest of your career, you gain each of your subclass’s features that are of your Mechanist level or lower.

### Level 4: Ability

### Score Improvement

You gain the Ability Score Improvement feat or another feat of your choice for which you qualify. You gain this feature again at Mechanist levels 8, 12, and 16.

### Level 5: Hard Reset

As a Bonus Action, you can cause your broken Apparatus within 30 feet of you to instantly become repaired and regain one lost Function. Once you use this feature, you can’t do so again until you finish a Long Rest.

### Level 6: Emergency Power

Once per day when you finish a Short Rest, you can regain a number of Power Charges equal to your Intelligence modifier (minimum 1).

### Level 10: Basic

### Material Alchemy

The AC of your Apparatus increases by one category (i.e. Wood to Stone - 15 to 17). This can’t affect iron, steel, or mithral. In addition, your Apparatus gains a +2 bonus to ability checks and saving throws.

### Level 11: Nicks n’ Scrapes

Your Apparatus now has a Damage Threshold equal to twice your Proficiency Bonus.

### Level 15: Sturdy Construction

Your Apparatus can now be primarily made from mithral and adamantine, adding the following to the bottom of the Apparatus Material table. Substance Armor Class Mithral 21 Adamantine 23

### Level 18: Advanced

### Material Alchemy

Your Basic Material Alchemy feature now can affect iron, steel, or mithral. In addition, if your active Apparatus didn’t lose a Function since your last turn while in combat, it restores one lost Function.

### Level 19: Epic Boon

You gain an Epic Boon feat or another feat of your choice for which you qualify.

### Level 20: Effortless

### Engineering

As an action with Tinker’s Tools in hand, you can choose to teleport in an Apparatus you’ve already made within the last 30 days, replacing any active or broken Apparatus you have. It appears exactly as it was created previously in an unoccupied space within 30 feet of you. Once you use this feature, you can’t do so again until you finish a Long Rest.

### Mechanist Subclasses

A Mechanist subclass is a specialization that grants you features at certain Mechanist levels, as specified in the subclass. This section presents the Builder, Engineer, and Magi-technician subclasses.

### Builder

Assemble Fortifications Unbreakable Mechanists who become Builders are focused on crafting the best defense. While they love tinkering, they prefer to work on adjustments or enhancements, and not repairing.

#### LEVEL 3: WALKING SHIELDS

You can strip a piece of your apparatus and create a protective fortification as a Bonus Action. The fortification is a Medium object that appears as a 10-foot tall, 1-foot thick bulwark that gives a +1 bonus to Armor Class to all allies within 5 feet of it. A Small or smaller creature can use this fortification, additionally, as Half Cover. The fortifications can rotate (costing no movement), and move along the ground using your movement during your turn. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### LEVEL 7: CAPSULE COMMAND

As an action while you can see your Apparatus within 60 feet of you, you can cause it to form a Sphere of protection around you from its materials, ceasing to be an Apparatus. The Sphere is a number of feet in radius equal to ten times your Proficiency Bonus and has the following effects. Armored Cover. The Sphere has an Armor Class equal to that of the Apparatus it was made from and grants ThreeQuarters Cover to any creature inside. Defensive Barrier. Attack rolls and nonmagical areas of effects can’t pass through the Sphere, though spells and other magical effects that don’t use attack rolls can. If an attack roll hits the Sphere or a nonmagical area of effect that deals damage overlaps with it, you must make an Intelligence check (DC 4 plus the number of times this has occurred to the Sphere before your next Short or Long Rest). On a failed check, the Sphere breaks apart, reforming in the nearest unoccupied space as your broken Apparatus. You can also choose to change the Sphere back into your broken Apparatus as a Bonus Action.

#### LEVEL 14: FORTIFYING WALLS

When you use your Walking Shields feature to create a protective fortification, you can choose to have it be a Large object that appears as a 10-foot tall, 1-foot thick wall panel. It now can be used as Half Cover by Medium or smaller creatures and can defy gravity to move in the air using your movement.

#### LEVEL 14: DUAL CHARGE

When you expend Power Charges to allow one Apparatus to use a Function, you can expend the requisite additional Power Charges as a Reaction to have your second Apparatus use a Function as part of the same action.

### Magi-Technician

Mix Magic and Mechanics Mechanists typically focus on science rather than the arcane, seeking to understand and overcome magical forces through technology. Magi-Technicians, however, are drawn to the mysteries of magic itself, finding ways to study it and discover how they, too, can wield its power.

### Engineer

Craft Double the Technological Trouble While all Mechanists love to tinker with their apparatus, the Engineer takes this passion further by mastering not one, but two devices at a time.

#### LEVEL 3: HARRIED SWARM

You can create two Apparatus instead of one. The size of each is dependent on what size your single Apparatus would be: Small (2 Tiny Apparatus), Medium (2 Small Apparatus), Large (2 Medium Apparatus), Huge (2 Large Apparatus), and Gargantuan (2 Huge Apparatus). A Tiny Apparatus created by this feature can be worn, but can be ridden only by a Tiny creature. Your two Apparatus gain the following benefits. One or the Other. A Function which allows you to cause an effect with your Apparatus can only involve one of the Apparatus (your choice). Tactical Advantage. Both Apparatus count as allied creatures to you, your allies, and each other. Flanking. When an Apparatus and at least one of its allies are adjacent to an enemy and on opposite sides or corners of the enemy’s space, each flanks that enemy. Each has Advantage on melee attack rolls against that enemy while it is flanked in this way. Doubled Reset. When you later gain the Hard Reset feature, it can affect both your Apparatus at the same time if both are within range.

#### LEVEL 7: BEACON OF ACCURACY

Your Apparatus becomes a beacon for ranged assaults. If you or an ally’s ranged attack roll can draw a 5-foot-wide Line through the space of at least one of your Apparatus, the range of the attack roll is increased by 20 feet. If the attack roll has a normal range and a long range, its normal range is increased by 20 feet. In addition, the attack ignores Half Cover and Three-Quarters Cover.

#### LEVEL 3: ARCANE KNOWHOW

You gain proficiency in Arcana. In addition, your Apparatus can be outfitted with rod, staff, and wand magic items as if they were adventuring gear. As a Magic action, you can have your Apparatus use those items.

#### LEVEL 3: MAGIC FUNCTIONS

You’ve learned how to recreate spells using pure science. Choose level 1+ spells from the Bard, Cleric, Druid, and Wizard spell lists. The spells must have a combined level equal to your Proficiency Bonus. You can cast these spells from your Apparatus using Magic Charges, which you can gain using Potion Power below and additional features at later levels. These charges are separate from the Power Charges your Apparatus usually uses. A spell costs a number of Magic Charges equal to its level. You can only have a number of Magic Charges at any one time equal to your Proficiency Bonus. Whenever you gain a Mechanist level, you can replace one of these spells with a new one. Once you have cast each spell once, you can’t do so again until you have finished a Long Rest. Potion Power. The easiest way to fuel your Apparatus’ spells is with magical potions, such as a Potion of Healing. As a Bonus Action while within 5 feet of your Apparatus, you can pour a potion into it, consuming the potion. You gain a number of Magic Charges based on the rarity of the potion, for each potion consumed in this way: Common (1), Uncommon (2), Rare (3), Very Rare or Legendary (A number of Magic Charges equal to its maximum).

#### LEVEL 7: MAGICAL ABSORPTION

Your understanding of magic science allows you to deconstruct magic into raw power which you can use to fuel your Apparatus’ magical functions. When a creature you can see within 30 feet of your Apparatus casts a spell with Verbal, Somatic, or Material components, you can take a Reaction to attempt to have your Apparatus absorb the spell. If the spell is of a level equal to your Proficiency Bonus or lower, it is absorbed with no effect, and the action, Bonus Action, or Reaction used to cast it is wasted. If the spell was cast with a spell slot, the slot is also expended. If the spell is of a level higher than your Proficiency Bonus, make an Intelligence check (DC 10 plus that spell’s level). On a successful check, the spell is absorbed and has no effect with the same effect as referenced above. When you absorb a spell in this way, you gain a number of Magic Charges equal to the spell’s level. Cantrips don’t provide any Magic Charges. You can also take an action to absorb active magic. Choose one creature, object, or magical effect within 30 feet of your Apparatus. Any spell of a level equal to your Proficiency Bonus or lower on the target ends. For each spell of a level higher than your Proficiency Bonus on the target, make an Intelligence check (DC 10 plus that spell’s level). On a successful check, the spell ends and you gain a number of Magic Charges equal to the spell’s level. This feature is treated as having the same effect as the Dispel Magic spell for other effects, creatures, or objects that reference the Dispel Magic spell, but isn’t itself magical. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### LEVEL 14: FUELED BY MAGIC

You have discovered how to convert magical power into energy for your Apparatus. Your Magic Charges can now additionally be expended for

### Functions

Functions You start with only access to Journeyman Functions, getting access to later Tiers as you gain Mechanist levels. Each Function shows its cost and if there are any benefits to spending additional Power Charges.

#### JOURNEYMAN FUNCTIONS

These are the basic initial Functions your Apparatus is capable of. WHIRLING CHAINS 1 Power Charge As an action, chains whirl about your Apparatus, giving Disadvantage to all attack rolls made within 5 feet of the Apparatus for 1 minute. SPRUNG SHIELD 1 Power Charge As a Reaction you take when another creature hits your Apparatus with an attack roll, you add 2 to your Apparatus’ Armor Class, including against the triggering attack, until the start of your next turn. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 2 Power Charges you spend on this Function, the bonus to AC increases by 1. HYPER BOOST 1 Power Charge As a Reaction, your Apparatus gains a boost to its mobility for 1 minute. When you first use this Function, and then as a Reaction on successive turns afterwards, your Apparatus gains an additional 15 feet of movement. SIDE SWIPERS 1 Power Charge As an action, chains, bars or plates spin off the sides of your Apparatus in a 5-foot Emanation until the end of your turn. Whenever the Emanation enters a creature’s space, that creature must succeed on a Dexterity saving throw or take 1d4 Slashing damage and have the Prone condition. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional Power Charge you spend on this Function, the damage increases by 1d4.

MECHANICAL BLADES 2 Power Charges As an action, blades momentarily spin or slide in and out of your Apparatus. All creatures within 5 feet of your Apparatus take 1d8 Slashing damage. You and your Apparatus have Advantage on the next attack roll against each creature that took this damage before the end of your next turn. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 2 Power Charges you spend on this Function, the damage increases by 1d8. SHOCK BUMPER 1 Power Charge Pistons push out slamming opponents with an electrically charged blunt object. As an action, your Apparatus makes a melee attack roll with a reach of 5 feet. It is considered proficient with it, and you add your Intelligence modifier to the attack roll and Bludgeoning damage roll. On a hit, the target takes 1d6 Bludgeoning damage plus 1d4 Lightning damage, and must succeed on a Constitution saving throw or be unable to take Reactions until the end of its next turn. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 2 Power Charges you spend on this Function, the Bludgeoning damage increases by 1d6 and the Lightning damage by 1d4. PULSE BOOSTER 1 Power Charge As a Reaction, your Apparatus can move up to 10 feet without provoking Opportunity Attacks. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 2 Power Charges you spend on this Function, the amount of feet your Apparatus can move increases by 5 feet. TRASH DUMP 1 Power Charge (Requires adventuring gear such as Caltrops or Ball Bearings) As a Utilize action, your Apparatus uses any combination of two of the adventuring gear it’s holding onto the ground in a 5-foot space within 15 feet of it. ALARM 1 Power Charge As an action, an alarm system is set into place around the Apparatus that lasts for 8 hours or until you turn it off. If a creature enters within 20-feet of the Apparatus while the alarm system is active, it produces a loud sound for 10 seconds, audible within 60 feet of the Apparatus. You can also designate that the alarm sends a signal to a portable device attached to you so long as you are within 1 mile of the Apparatus. SHOCK HOOK 2 Power Charges Lightning springs from the Apparatus. As an action, your Apparatus makes a melee attack roll with a reach of 15 feet. It is considered proficient with it, and you add your Intelligence modifier to the attack roll and damage roll. On a hit, the target takes 1d8 Lightning damage, and it can’t make Opportunity Attacks until the start of its next turn. In addition, the target must succeed on a Strength saving throw or be pulled up to 10 feet in a straight line toward the Apparatus. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 2 Power Charges you spend on this Function, the damage increases by 1d8.

WIND BARRIER 2 Power Charges FLAME THROWER 1 Power Charge As an action, turbines exhale wind at high speeds all around the Apparatus in a 5-foot Emanation. Whenever the Emanation enters a creature’s space and whenever a creature enters the Emanation or ends its turn there, it must succeed on a Strength saving throw or be pushed up to 5 feet away from the Apparatus. A creature makes this save only once per turn. In addition, the strong wind keeps fog, smoke, and other gases at bay. Small or smaller flying creatures or objects can’t pass through the Emanation. Loose, lightweight materials brought into the Emanation fly upward. Arrows, bolts, and other ordinary projectiles launched at the Apparatus are deflected upward and miss automatically. Boulders hurled by Giants or siege engines, and similar projectiles, are unaffected. Creatures in gaseous form can’t pass through it. These effects last until the start of your next turn, but you can spend an additional Power Charge at the start of your turn to maintain the effects (no action required). As an action, a thin sheet of flames shoots forth from your Apparatus in a 15-foot Cone. Flammable objects within the Cone that aren’t being worn or carried start burning. Each creature in the area makes a Dexterity saving throw, taking 2d8 Fire damage and starts burning on a failed save or half as much damage only on a successful one. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 2 Power Charges you spend on this Function, the damage increases by 1d8. MAGNETIC CUSHION 1 Power Charge As a Bonus Action, the Apparatus can jump up to 30 feet by spending 10 feet of movement. If it would take damage from the jump’s fall, it takes no damage instead. Alternatively, as a Reaction when the Apparatus is falling, it takes no damage from the fall.

FORCE GUN 1 Power Charge As an action, your Apparatus fires three bolts of energy. Each bolt targets a creature of your choice that you can see within 120 feet. Your Apparatus makes a ranged attack roll for each bolt. It is considered proficient with it, and you add your Intelligence modifier to the attack rolls and damage rolls. On a hit, the target takes 1d4 Force damage. Alternatively, your Apparatus can spray shots in a 10-foot Cube within 60 feet. Each creature in that area must succeed on a Dexterity saving throw or take 1d4 Force damage. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 2 Power Charges you spend on this Function, the damage increases by 1d4. it moves. If lit, the oil burns until the end of the turn 2 rounds from when the oil was lit (or 12 seconds) and deals 5 Fire damage to any creature that enters the area or ends its turn there. A creature can take this damage only once per turn. PROPELLERS 1 Power Charge As an action, your Apparatus gains a Fly Speed of 30 feet and can hover for 1 minute. The target falls if it is still aloft unless it can stop the fall. You can spend an additional Power Charge at the end of each minute to maintain the effects (no action required).

#### EXPERT FUNCTIONS

These are more advanced Functions your Apparatus can be configured with. ANCHORED 1 Power Charge As a Bonus Action, your Apparatus plants itself down to the ground, reducing its movement to 0 and giving it Advantage on all saving throws against being moved. If your Apparatus does fail such a saving throw, it can’t be moved more than 5 feet. It can release itself from the ground as a Bonus Action, ending this effect. OIL TRAIL 1 Power Charge (Requires Oil) As a Bonus Action, your Apparatus releases the oil within it on level ground, covering a 1-foot-wide Line up to 30-feet-long as

HARPOON 2 Power Charges As an action, your Apparatus makes a ranged attack roll with a range of 30 feet. It is considered proficient with it, and you add your Intelligence modifier to the attack roll and damage roll. On a hit, the target takes 1d8 Piercing damage and is Grappled by your Apparatus. When the Grappled target is moved by your Apparatus due to the grapple, it must succeed on a Dexterity saving throw or take 1d4 Bludgeoning damage for every 5 feet it is moved. A creature takes 1d4 Slashing damage if it escapes from the grapple, or if it tries and fails. Your Apparatus can grapple only one creature at a time with its harpoon. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 2 Power Charges you spend on this Function, the initial Piercing damage increases by 1d8. STEEL NET 1 Power Charge As an action, your Apparatus launches a steel net at a creature you can see within 30 feet of it. The target must succeed on a Dexterity saving throw or have the Restrained condition until it escapes. The target succeeds automatically if it is Huge or larger. To escape, the target or a creature within 5 feet of it must take an action to make a Strength (Athletics) check (DC equals your Function save DC), freeing the Restrained creature on a success. Destroying the Net (AC 19; 18 HP; Immunity to Bludgeoning, Poison, and Psychic damage) also frees the target, ending the Restrained condition on it. RAM 1 Power Charge As a Bonus Action, your Apparatus protrudes a heavy ram until the end of your turn. It gains a +4 bonus and Advantage on Strength checks used to break down doors or other obstructions to a doorway. In addition, immediately after the Apparatus hits a creature within 5 feet of it with a melee attack as part of the Attack action while the ram is protruding and the target is no more than one size larger than it, the target is pushed 10 feet away and has the Prone condition.

ENCLOSED SHIELD 1 Power Charge LIGHTNING CANNONS 2 Power Charges As a Bonus Action, your Apparatus gains 10 Temporary Hit Points for 1 minute. If it takes damage while these Temporary Hit Points remain, it doesn’t lose a Function. Your apparatus fires two bolts of lightning, each forming a 50-foot-long, 5-foot-wide Line in two different directions you choose. Each creature in the Lines makes a Dexterity saving throw, taking 2d6 Lightning damage on a failed save or half as much damage on a successful one. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 2 Power Charges you spend on this Function, the damage increases by 2d6. STEEL SKIN 2 Power Charges As an action, your Apparatus reinforces itself against damage for 1 minute. When your Apparatus takes Bludgeoning, Piercing, or Slashing damage while this Function is active, you have Advantage on Intelligence checks to prevent Functions from being lost. WATER BREATHING SPHERE 1 Power Charge As an action, your Apparatus emits a force field that eschews water in a 15-foot Emanation for 1 hour. You can spend an additional Power Charge at the end of each hour to maintain the effects (no action required).

WHIRLING FLAMES 2 Power Charges HUNKER DOWN 4 Power Charges Whirling flames wreathe your Apparatus for 1 minute, shedding Bright Light in a 10-foot radius and Dim Light for an additional 10 feet. Whenever a creature within 5 feet of your Apparatus hits it with a melee attack roll, the attacker takes 1d8 Fire damage. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 2 Power Charges you spend on this Function, the damage increases by 1d8. A force barrier encloses your Apparatus until the start of your next turn. Nothing—not physical objects, energy, or other spell effects—can pass through the barrier, in or out, though a creature in the barrier can breathe there. The barrier is immune to all damage, and anything inside can’t be damaged by attacks or effects originating from outside, nor can anything inside the barrier damage anything outside it. The barrier is weightless and just large enough to contain your Apparatus and a creature wearing or riding it. A Disintegrate spell targeting the barrier destroys it without harming anything inside. SLICK SERVOS 2 Power Charges As an action, your Apparatus’ mobility systems go into overdrive for 1 minute. Your Apparatus’ movement is unaffected by Difficult Terrain, and spells and other magical effects can neither reduce its Speed nor cause it to have the Paralyzed or Restrained conditions. Your Apparatus also has a Swim Speed equal to its Speed. In addition, your Apparatus can spend 5 feet of movement to automatically escape from nonmagical restraints, such as a net or a creature imposing the Grappled condition on it. TRUNK OF HOLDING 1 Power Charge Your Apparatus gains a hidden compartment that is magnetically locked unless you spend 1 Power Charge (the cost listed above) to open it, in which case it remains open for 10 minutes before closing again. The compartment can hold a number of pounds dependant on the Apparatus’ size (Small: 50 pounds, Medium: 100 pounds, Large: 200 pounds, Huge: 500 pounds, Gargantuan: 800 pounds), not exceeding a volume of cubic feet depending on the Apparatus’ size (Small: 1, Medium: 2, Large: 5, Huge: 10, Gargantuan: 20). Retrieving an item from the compartment requires a Utilize action.

#### ILLUSTRIOUS FUNCTIONS

These are even more advanced Functions your Apparatus can be configured with. NITRO 2 Power Charges A burst of energy supes up your Apparatus. For the next minute, as a Bonus Action, your Apparatus takes the Dash action. ANTIMATTER CANNON 2 Power Charges As an action, your Apparatus launches a ray at a target you can see within 60 feet. The target can be a creature or a nonmagical object. A creature targeted by the ray makes a Dexterity saving throw. On a failed save, the target takes 6d8 + 10 Necrotic damage. If this damage reduces it to 0 Hit Points, it and everything nonmagical it is wearing and carrying cease to exist. The target can be revived only by a True Resurrection or Wish spell. The ray automatically removes a Large or smaller nonmagical object or a creation of magical force. If such a target is Huge or larger, the ray removes a 10-foot Cube portion of it. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 1 Power Charge you spend on this Function, the damage increases by 1d8 + 5. AUTO-FIRE 2 Power Charges Your Apparatus fires up an auto-firing mechanism for its ammunition. As a Bonus Action, your Apparatus can make two of its ranged attack rolls. This Function is active until the start of your next turn, but you can spend an additional Power Charge at the start of your turn to maintain it (no action required). GROUND SLAM 2 Power Charges As an action, an intense tremor rips through the ground in a 50-foot-radius circle centered on your Apparatus. The ground there is Difficult Terrain. Each creature on the ground in the area makes a Dexterity saving throw. On a failed save, a creature has the Prone condition and its Concentration is broken. You can also cause the effects below. Fissures. A total of 1d4 fissures open in the area at the end of the turn you use it. You choose the fissures’ locations, which can’t be under structures. Each fissure is 1d6 × 5 feet deep and 5 feet wide, and it extends from one edge of the area to another edge. A creature in the same space as a fissure must succeed on a Dexterity saving throw or fall in. A creature that successfully saves moves with the fissure’s edge as it opens. Structures. The tremor deals 25 Bludgeoning damage to any structure in contact with the ground in the area when you use this Function. If a structure drops to 0 Hit Points, it collapses. A creature within a distance from a collapsing structure equal to half the structure’s height makes a Dexterity saving throw. On a failed save, the creature takes 12d6 Bludgeoning damage, has the Prone condition, and is buried in the rubble, requiring a DC 20 Strength (Athletics) check as an action to escape. On a successful save, the creature takes half as much damage only. This Function is active until the start of your next turn, but you can spend an additional Power Charge at the start of your turn to maintain it (no action required). Each creature on the ground in the area must repeat the Dexterity saving throw or have the Prone condition and have its Concentration broken, and structures take the damage again.

PINNED PLATES 2 Power Charges DESTRUCTIVE BLAST 2 Power Charge As a Bonus Action taken immediately after the Apparatus hits a creature with a melee attack roll as part of the Attack action and the target is no more than one size larger than it, the target has the Prone condition, and it has Restrained condition until the end of its next turn. As an action, destructive energy ripples outward from your Apparatus in a 30-foot Emanation. Each creature in the Emanation makes a Constitution saving throw. On a failed save, a target takes 3d6 Thunder damage and 3d6 Radiant or Necrotic damage (your choice) and has the Prone condition. On a successful save, a target takes half as much damage only. Spending Additional Charges. For each additional 2 Power Charges you spend on this Function, both forms of damage increase by 1d6. POISON CLOUD 2 Power Charges (Requires Basic Poison) As a Bonus Action, your Apparatus distills poison into a cloud that it emits in a 20-foot Emanation of poisonous gas. The cloud is Heavily Obscured. The cloud lingers in the air until the end of your turn or until a strong wind (such as the one created by Gust of Wind) disperses it. Whenever the Emanation enters a creature’s space and whenever a creature enters the Emanation or ends its turn there, the creature must succeed on a Constitution saving throw or have the Poisoned condition until the end of its next turn. While Poisoned in this way, the creature can’t take an action or a Bonus Action. A creature makes this save only once per turn. INSTANT COVER 1 Power Charge As a Bonus Action, your Apparatus gains Three-Quarters Cover. As soon as your Apparatus moves, it loses the cover benefit. TRANSMIT MESSAGE 1 Power Charge As an action, your Apparatus can send a short message of your creation that is 30 words or fewer to any device which can receive transmission across any distance. The recipient can then immediately send a short answering transmission in like manner. AERIAL ROCKET 4 Power Charges As an action, your Apparatus protrudes rockets for 1 hour. While the rockets are protruding, the Apparatus has a Fly Speed of 150 feet and can hover, and it has Immunity to the Prone condition. The only actions the Apparatus can take while the rockets are protruding are the Dash action or an action to retract the rockets. Retracting lasts until the end of your next turn, during which the Apparatus has the Stunned condition. You can spend an additional Power Charge at the end of each hour to maintain the effects (no action required).

#### GENIUS FUNCTIONS

These are highly advanced Functions your Apparatus can be configured with. BLADE BARRAGE 4 Power Charges Each target makes a Dexterity saving throw, taking 10d8 Lightning damage on a failed save or half as much damage on a successful one. Spending Additional Charges. For each 2 additional Power Charges you spend on this Function, one additional bolt leaps from the first target to another target. As an action, whirling blades swing outward from your Apparatus in a 5-foot Emanation. Its space is Difficult Terrain. Any creature in the Emanation makes a Dexterity saving throw, taking 6d10 Slashing damage on a failed save or half as much damage on a successful one. A creature also makes that save if it enters the Emanation or ends its turn there. A creature makes that save only once per turn. This Function is active until the start of your next turn, but you can spend 2 additional Power Charges at the start of your turn to maintain it (no action required). ANTI-MAGIC BARRIER 4 Power Charges As an action, your Apparatus emits a shimmering barrier in a 5-foot Emanation around it. Any spell of a level equal to your Proficiency Bonus or lower cast from outside the barrier can’t affect anything within it. Such a spell can target creatures and objects within the barrier, but the spell has no effect on them. Similarly, the area within the barrier is excluded from areas of effect created by such spells. This function is active until the start of your next turn, but you can spend 2 additional Power Charges at the start of your turn to maintain it (no action required).

#### SUPER GENIUS FUNCTIONS

These are even more highly advanced Functions your Apparatus can be configured with. SPYGLASS OF OMENS 2 Power Charges As an action, your Apparatus has Truesight with a range of 60 feet for 1 minute, and it transmits signals to you via gear you are wearing to allow you to be aware of what it sees. ARCING ENERGY 4 Power Charges As an action, your Apparatus launches a bolt of energy toward a target you can see within 80 feet. Three bolts then leap from that target to as many as three other targets of your choice, each of which must be within 15 feet of the first target. A target can be a creature or an object and can be targeted by only one of the bolts.

### Subclasses

The following subclasses are designed for use in any setting, but have their roots built based on characters and plotlines seen in the ThunderCats universe. Characters can be built using any subclass in this or other publications, but when playing ThunderCats adventures, the subclasses below are designed to feel like a perfect fit: Path of Collision: A Barbarian of explosive force in motion, shaking the battlefield with sheer force. College of Omens: A Bard gifted with Sight Beyond Sight to gain prophetic insight in combat and social encounters. Ancient Spirits Domain: A Cleric who walks the razor’s edge of good and evil, granting power to allies or themselves. Circle of the Croft: A Druid attuned to unicorns, able to summon their forms with radiant, moonlit magic. Operator: A Fighter of adaptability, able to modify weapons and armor to meet any challenge. Warrior of the Claw: A Monk like an agile predator, leaping great distances and pouncing with feline ferocity. Oath of Destiny: A Paladin oathbound to a path of fate neither they nor their foes can deny. Evil-Chaser: A Ranger sworn to hunt evil, striking terror in corrupted hearts. Heckler: A Rogue who disorients foes with taunts and cutting wit, leaving enemies frustrated and distracted. Metamorphosis Sorcery: A Sorcerer infused with magical mummification, tapping into unworldly strength and transforming into monstrous forms of terrible strength. Mongor Patron: A Warlock bound to the demon of fear, channeling and amplifying dread itself. Magician: A Wizard of deception, bending space and perception through subtle spellcraft and hidden portals.

### Path of Collision (Barbarian)

Charge Forth with Unstoppable Force Barbarians do not follow the Path of Collision lightly; they use it as a runway to charge headfirst into the enemy! These Barbarians use mobility and momentum to their and their allies’ benefit, throwing their foes off balance while refusing to let anything get in between them and their target.

#### LEVEL 3: STURDY FRAME

Armor is considered to be one category lighter than it is while you are wearing it. Heavy armor is considered to be Medium armor, Medium armor is considered to be Light, and while wearing actual Light armor you can calculate your base Armor Class using the armor and still gain the benefit of features that reference not wearing armor. For example, your Unarmored Defense feature would use the base AC of the Light armor you are wearing, plus your Dexterity and Constitution modifiers. In addition, creatures have Disadvantage on saving throws made to resist you using the Shove option of an Unarmed Strike.

#### LEVEL 3: RAGEFUL RAM

Your Rage lets you rush forward like a runaway cargo train. While your Rage is active, you can choose to use the following additional rules when you take the Dash action on your turn: • The extra movement you gain from the Dash action can only be used to move in a straight line. • All Opportunity Attacks targeting you while moving in a straight line this turn are made at Disadvantage. • You make a number of melee attacks while moving in a straight line this turn equal to your Proficiency Bonus; up to one attack per target (creature or object). You don’t add your ability modifier to the damage rolls of these attacks.

#### LEVEL 6: ANCHORED WHIP

By planting your heels, grabbing an ally, and whipping them forward, you can hurl them with surprising force. When an ally starts its turn within 5 feet of you, you can take a Reaction to let the ally move up to its Speed in a straight line. This movement uses the rules from your Rageful Ram feature.

#### LEVEL 10: HEADLONG CHARGE

Once on your turn, at the end of any movement that is 20+ feet in a straight line, you can make one melee attack roll (no action required). After the attack resolves, you have the Prone condition.

#### LEVEL 14: UNSTOPPABLE

#### MOMENTUM

Once you push yourself into motion, woe be to anything that gets in your way. While your Rage is active, you have Immunity to the Grappled, Prone, and Restrained conditions, and are immune to being forcibly pushed or pulled. In addition, any melee attack that benefits from your Rage Damage bonus also applies the Shove option of an Unarmed Strike.

### College of Omens (Bard)

Weave Sights Beyond with Wisdom and Song Bards of the College of Omens believe they see the worlds around them in the light of what has happened, what is happening, and what has yet to happen. They are storytellers and singers of songs that speak of the passage of time, and the cyclical nature of how it interacts with the universe. They use this special sight beyond sight to help them and their allies through the most difficult of situations, casting portents and learning from the past to aid the future.

#### LEVEL 3: IMBUE THE SIGHT

You are always considered to have the Sight Beyond Sight spell (a spell a chapter 1) prepared. You can cast this spell once without expending a spell slot, but must finish a Long Rest before you can do so again.

#### LEVEL 3: WORDS OF WISDOM

When you give a creature a Bardic Inspiration die, you can choose a Divination spell you have prepared and infuse it with that die. This doesn’t expend a spell slot. The level of the spell is determined by the die’s size, as shown on the Infused Inspiration table. The creature can expend the Bardic Inspiration die to cast the chosen spell. It uses Charisma as the spellcasting ability for this casting, and the spell’s normal casting time applies. INFUSED INSPIRATION Bardic Inspiration Die Words of Wisdom Spell Level 1d6 1 1d8 1–3 1d10 1–5 1d12 1–7

#### LEVEL 6: OPEN MIND AND EYES

While a creature has one of your Bardic Inspiration dice, you gain the following benefits. Perception Checks. You can use that creature’s Perception modifier in place of your own for any Wisdom (Perception) check that relies on hearing or sight. Sense Sharing. As a Bonus Action, you can see through that creature’s eyes and hear what it hears until the start of your next turn. During this time, you gain the benefits of any special senses (Blindsight, Darkvision, etc.) the creature has. Spellcasting Origin. When you cast a spell, you can choose to cast it as if you were in that creature’s space. The creature must be within 100 feet of you. Telepathic Link. If that creature is within 100 feet of you, you can communicate with it telepathically.

#### LEVEL 14: SIGHTS INTO FUTURES YET

#### WRITTEN

Whenever a creature uses one of your Bardic Inspiration dice, it can roll that Bardic Inspiration die twice and choose the higher result. When a creature with one of your Bardic Inspiration dice fails a D20 Test, you can take a Reaction to gain insight into the task. You immediately learn the DC (or the target’s AC if the failed D20 Test was an attack roll), and what consequence or effect follows from the failed D20 Test, if any (for example, a harmful effect or condition will occur).

### Ancient Spirits Domain (Cleric)

Channel the Ancient Spirits of Good and Evil The Ancient Spirits Domain is a forged relationship between a Cleric and the infinitely powerful entities that thrive on the rise and fall of morality in the universe. There are some that dedicate themselves to one side of the moral divide, eventually vying to become a scion or avatar for their chosen Spirits, but most of their loyal followers understand that achieving balance between the two can be just as powerful. The Ancient Spirits of Evil and Good are not deities as much as they are forces of nature in the ThunderCats universe. There are four unique spirits with versions on both sides of the moral division, representing the four supporting pillars of ideology that carries the whole. Individual cultures or communities might have different specific names for them, but on Third Earth, they are defined as War, Spirit, Tech, and Soul. Followers of the Ancient Spirits know that they can devote actions or even eras of their life to one over the others, but to unlock their full potential, all four must be invoked through words and deeds alike. result of 1 or 2 on any of your damage dice, but must use the new roll. Good. When you initially choose Good, each creature of your choice within 20 feet of you (excluding yourself) gains Temporary Hit Points equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of 1) plus your Proficiency Bonus and is affected by the Lesser Restoration spell. While active, you can reroll a result of 1 or 2 on any dice you roll to restore Hit Points, but must use the new roll.

#### LEVEL 3: INVOKE THE FOUR

You always have the Protection From Evil and Good spell prepared, and can cast it as a Reaction without expending a

#### SPELLS

to make a saving throw. When you cast it in this way, the spell lasts until the end of your next turn instead of its normal duration (no Concentration required). You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### LEVEL 3: ANCIENT SPIRITS DOMAIN

SPELLS Your connection to this spiritual domain ensures you always have certain spells ready. When you reach a Cleric level specified in the Ancient Spirits Domain Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared. ANCIENT SPIRITS DOMAIN SPELLS Cleric Level Spells 3 Bane, Bless, Suggestion, Zone of Truth 5 Bestow Curse, Remove Curse 7 Blight, Death Ward 9 Dispel Evil and Good, Form of the EverLiving (a spell in Chapter 1)

#### LEVEL 3: CHANNEL SPIRITS

When you choose this domain you attune yourself to powerful spiritual forces of Good and Evil. You immediately choose one of these spirits to be your primary leaning. As a Bonus Action, you can expend one use of your Channel Divinity to switch your leaning between Evil and Good or vice versa. You can also change your choice when you finish a Long Rest. Your chosen spirits remain active until you change them. Evil. When you initially choose Evil, you gain Temporary Hit Points equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum 1) plus your Proficiency Bonus and are affected by the Lesser Restoration spell. While active, you can reroll a

#### LEVEL 6: ENHANCED FORM

You have enhanced your connection to the Ancient Spirits. This gives you an additional effect you can enact when using your Channel Spirits feature. Evil. When you would normally roll one or more dice to deal damage to a creature with a spell or Channel Divinity, you maximize your damage dice against each target. For example, instead of dealing 2d6 damage, you deal 12. Good. When you would normally roll one or more dice to restore Hit Points to a creature with a spell or Channel Divinity, you maximize each die’s result for each target. For example, instead of restoring 2d6 Hit Points to a creature, you restore 12. Once you use this feature, you can’t do so again until you finish a Long Rest. You can also restore your use of it by expending a use of your Channel Divinity (no action required).

#### LEVEL 17: THE EVER-LIVING

As a Magic action, you can take on a portion of the “everliving” form the Ancient Spirits can bestow by expending a use of your Channel Divinity. Depending on your current leaning toward Evil or Good, you gain all of the normal benefits of your Channel Spirits feature as well as the following additional benefit. Evil. You regain a number of expended spell slots levels equal to your Proficiency Bonus, starting with your lowest expended spell levels. In addition, you can concentrate on two different Concentration spells, provided each spell has a range of Self. If you are concentrating on only one spell, you have Advantage on Constitution saving throws you make to maintain Concentration on it. If you lose Concentration while concentrating on two spells, you choose which spell you lose Concentration on. Good. You regain a number of expended spell slot levels equal to your Proficiency Bonus, starting with your highest expended spell levels. In addition, each creature of your choice has the Empowered condition while it remains within 20 feet of you. While active, each creature of your choice (excluding you) within 20 feet of you regains 1 Hit Point at the start of each of its turns. This Ever-Living state lasts for a number of minutes equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum 1). When the effects end, you can immediately expend another use of your Channel Divinity to begin the Ever-Living state again (no action required). When the effects of this feature end, and you don’t choose to begin the Ever-Living state again, you gain 1 Exhaustion level and the Pacified condition until you finish a Short or Long Rest.

### Circle of the Croft (Druid)

Call Upon the Light of Unicorns The Circle of the Croft is a powerful connection between the Druid and one of Third Earth’s most regal and majestic beasts of legend: the unicorn. Combining their own mystic nature with that of the great horned stallions and mares, the Croft dedicants summon help from the herd of the moon and their mythic, pure light energies to aid their allies.

#### LEVEL 3: CIRCLE OF THE CROFT SPELLS

When you reach a Druid level specified in the Circle of the Croft Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared. In addition, you can cast the spells from this feature while you’re in a Wild Shape form. CIRCLE OF THE CROFT SPELLS Druid Level Prepared Spells 3 Aid, Cure Wounds, Faerie Fire, Lesser Restoration 5 Dispel Magic 7 Dominate Beast 9 Tree Stride

#### LEVEL 3: UNICORN SPIRIT

As a Magic action, you expend a use of your Wild Shape feature to summon a Large radiant, spiritual unicorn in a space you can see within 30 feet. The Unicorn Spirit lasts for the duration of Wild Shape or until you use Wild Shape again. The Unicorn Spirit shares your movement on your turn, effectively splitting your Speed in movement between you both in whatever ratio you choose. The Unicorn Spirit can’t be targeted for any reason and is otherwise intangible. Once summoned, you can take a Bonus Action to have the Unicorn Spirit emit an aura in a 10-foot Emanation, which gives off Bright Light within it and Dim Light for an additional 10 feet. The aura normally ends at the end of your next turn, but you can maintain its effects automatically by taking a Bonus Action. Each creature of your choice in the aura is affected by the Calm Emotions, Entangle, or Pass Without Trace spell (your choice when you choose to have Unicorn Spirit emit an aura). If the spell requires a saving throw to avoid its effects, the creature must make the saving throw as normal. If a creature succeeds a saving throw against an aura’s effects, it is immune to that aura’s effect until you finish a Long Rest.

#### LEVEL 6: SPIRITUAL HERD

#### LEVEL 10: UNICORN’S PROTECTION

When you use your Unicorn Spirit feature, you now conjure forth a number of Unicorn Spirits up to your Proficiency Bonus. All of the Unicorn Spirits must be summoned in spaces you can see within 30 feet. When you share movement with one Unicorn Spirit, the remaining Unicorn Spirits can move up to 10 feet. A single Unicorn Spirit can be expended as a Bonus Action to release one of the following effects. • Cure Wounds on a single target within 10 feet of the expended Unicorn Spirit. • Lesser Restoration on a single target within 10 feet of the expended Unicorn Spirit. • Moonbeam centered on the expended Unicorn Spirit, lasting until the end of your next turn (no Concentration required). In addition, anytime a Unicorn Spirit is expended for any reason, all poisons and toxins within 10 feet of the expended Unicorn Spirit are purified and are rendered harmless and inert. This also ends the Poisoned condition on targets within 10 feet of the expended Unicorn Spirit. You and allies within 10 feet of one or more of your Unicorn Spirits gain the effects of the Comprehend Languages spell, and a +1 bonus to Armor Class. In addition, you can now expend one of your Unicorn Spirits as a Reaction to the trigger of the Feather Fall or Shield spell to target any creature that was benefitting from its aura before it was expended with that spell.

#### LEVEL 14: GREATER UNICORN SPIRITS

The Unicorn Spirits you summon are enhanced. All ranges of Unicorn Spirit auras and effects when expended are doubled, and allies under the effects of a Unicorn Spirit aura have Advantage on all saving throws against spells and magical effects. In addition, you can choose to expend a Unicorn Spirit to cast Aura of Life, Confusion, or Hypnotic Pattern without expending a spell slot, centered on the space where the Unicorn Spirit was expended.

### Operator (Fighter)

Upgrade Armaments to Peak Efficiency Operators are Fighters focused on making the most out of their equipment and the energies that power them. They are master modifiers and tinkerers with how their gear functions, spending time coming up with new and interesting ways to apply their skills and talents through their weapon, armor, and vehicle assets.

#### LEVEL 3: GEAR EFFICIENCY

You are always more efficient in the utility functions of your equipment. You gain the following benefits: • Creatures have Disadvantage on saving throws made to avoid your weapons’ mastery properties. • The max bonus to Armor Class gained by your Dexterity modifier is increased by 1 on Medium armor you are wearing. Heavy armor you wear can benefit from your Dexterity modifier (max 1). • You can add your Proficiency Bonus to the Maximum Charges and Component Slot values of your Personal Vehicles. • Change the weapon mastery or damage type of one of your weapons. The damage type can be Bludgeoning, Fire, Lightning, Piercing, or Slashing damage. • The armor you are wearing makes you immune to the effects of mastery properties and being forcibly pushed, and gives you Advantage on one type of saving throw. • Increase the attack rolls and damage rolls from a single Weapon System on your Personal Vehicle by a value equal to your Proficiency Bonus. The effects of your Personal Modifications last until you finish a Short or Long Rest. In addition, objects affected by Personal Modifications only use these additional effects while in your possession, unless a different creature uses Heroic Inspiration for any reason while touching the object, which allows it to use the additional effects for 10 minutes.

#### LEVEL 7: EMPOWER

You can additionally choose Force or Radiant for the damage types listed in your Personal Modifications features. In addition, you can expend a use of your Second Wind to allow a nonmagical object or vehicle you are in contact with to regain a number of charges equal to your Proficiency Bonus.

#### LEVEL 3: PERSONAL MODIFICATIONS

#### LEVEL 10: BRILLIANT MODIFICATIONS

You gain Proficiency in Tinker’s Tools. You can alter the functionality of your equipment with a few spins and taps of your Tinker’s Tools. Either over the course of a Short Rest, or by expending a use of your Second Wind to do so as an Utilize action while holding Tinker’s Tools, you can apply one of the following effects: When you apply effects to an object using your Personal Modifications feature, you may additionally choose one of the following effects to apply: • Add a mastery property of your choice to one of your weapons; both will apply when attacking with that weapon. • The armor you are wearing gives you Resistance to one damage type or your choice. • All damage dice rolled for your Personal Vehicle Maneuvers rerolls all results of 1 or 2. You must use the new roll.

#### LEVEL 15: INGENIOUSLY INVENTIVE

Whenever you would apply one effect from your Personal Modifications feature, you can choose to apply up to two; either two to the same object, or one each to two different objects.

#### LEVEL 18: MASTERY AND PERFECTION

Objects benefiting from your Personal Modifications feature gain additional effects: • Weapons score a Critical Hit on a roll of 19 or 20 on the d20. • Armor you are wearing causes any Critical Hit against you to become a normal hit. • Attack rolls made by a Personal Vehicle are made with Advantage.

### Warrior of the Claw (Monk)

Brandish Claws and Champion Agility Warriors of the Claw focus on their own physicality, flexibility, and hand to hand combat, particularly the use of the clawed hand or foot. These Monks leap, rebound, and gymnastically move about the battlefield with ease, but do not do so at the cost of their own ferocity.

#### LEVEL 3: CLAWED STRIKES

If you don’t already possess them naturally, you grow your nails into claws with which you can make an Unarmed Strike. If you hit with this Unarmed Strike and deal damage, you can deal Slashing damage equal to 1d6 plus your Strength modifier, instead of the normal damage of an Unarmed Strike. Once per turn, you can add your Wisdom modifier (minimum 1) to the damage roll of an Unarmed Strike you make using your claws.

#### LEVEL 3: POUNCE

#### LEVEL 6: CLIMBING CLAWS

You gain a Climb Speed equal to your Speed. While climbing, you have the ability to move up, down, and across vertical surfaces and along ceilings, while leaving your hands free. In addition while climbing, Opportunity Attacks have Disadvantage against you.

#### LEVEL 11: UNTOUCHABLE

#### MANEUVERABILITY

When you move, you are considered to be gymnastically leaping across the ground or through the air. You can effectively teleport anywhere within your movement, but you must be able to have physically moved there. This allows you to avoid obstacles and enemies, but not pass through closed portals, between tight bars, or similar physical restrictions.

#### LEVEL 17: CLAWED GRASP ON FATE

You have a spiritual hold on fate with the same relentlessness as you do anything else caught in your claws. You can spend a Focus Point to automatically succeed on any saving throw without rolling (no action required). You can pounce forward with momentary bursts of motion. You gain the following benefits: • You can add up to 10 feet to any of your jump distances. • When you end a 10+ foot jump within 5 feet of an enemy, you can spend a Focus Point to make a special pounce Unarmed Strike attack against it. This is treated as an Unarmed Strike that deals damage and applies both the Topple and Vex mastery property effects. You can choose to use your Wisdom modifier instead of your Strength or Dexterity to calculate the save DC of the Topple mastery property used in this way.

#### LEVEL 6: RAKE OR REND

You can attack with the claws on your hands and feet interchangeably to devastating effect. You gain the following benefits: • When you make an Unarmed Strike as a Bonus Action using your Martial Arts feature, you can make an additional special raking Unarmed Strike, choosing the Damage option. On a hit, you roll two Martial Arts dice for damage, taking the lower result rolled. • When you take the Attack action, you can choose to forgo all other attack rolls for the turn (excluding attack rolls using Flurry of Blows) to instead make a special rending Unarmed Strike, choosing the Damage option. On a hit, the target takes damage equal to four rolls of your Martial Arts die and you apply the Sap mastery property effect.

### Oath of Destiny (Paladin)

Embody the Certainty of Your Destined Greatness The Oath of Destiny is more of a duty for a Paladin to claim than necessarily a set of adopted ideals. It is an acceptance of the fated glory or greatness that has been revealed or thrust upon the Paladin. They have a future laid out in front of them, and they have sworn to work toward its ultimate fulfillment. Those who take on the Oath of Destiny have somehow been informed of a great cause or future that calls to them. They know they are supposed to fill a specific role, perform a specific deed, or continue to uphold some kind of greater cause. They do not shy away from this, and instead draw power from their role in their destiny. These Paladins share the following tenets: • You must lead through action, as others will draw from your example. • Believe in yourself and the necessity of your future. • Let nothing stand in the way of your epic future.

#### LEVEL 3: OATH OF DESTINY SPELLS

The magic of your oath ensures you always have certain spells ready; when you reach a Paladin level specified in the Oath of Destiny Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared. OATH OF DESTINY SPELLS Paladin Level Prepared Spells 3 Bless, Divine Favor 5 Enhance Ability, Magic Weapon 9 Beacon of Hope, Magic Circle 15 Death Ward, Freedom of Movement 17 Dispel Evil and Good, Hallow

#### LEVEL 3: POWER OF DESTINY

As a Reaction, you can expend one use of your Channel Divinity to imbue a Destiny Point into yourself or an ally you can see within 30 feet. This Reaction may be taken at the beginning of the target’s turn, or in response to one of the triggers listed in the benefits below. A creature may only possess 1 Destiny Point at a time, and loses an unspent Destiny Point when it begins a Long Rest. A creature with a Destiny Point can expend it to enact one of the following benefits based on its trigger (no action required): • When the creature is reduced to 0 Hit Points but not killed outright, it can drop to 1 Hit Point instead. • When the creature suffers a Critical Hit, it can cause it to become a normal hit. • When the creature rolls a 19 on a D20 Test, it can treat the result as rolling a 20 on the d20. In addition, a creature with a Destiny Point can expend it at any time to give itself Heroic Inspiration if it doesn’t already have it (no action required).

#### LEVEL 7: AURA OF FATE

Each ally that starts its turn in your aura gains the Proficiency and Speed Bonus effect of the Empowered condition; it doesn’t actually gain the condition. The ally loses the benefits of this effect if it starts its turn outside the aura. In addition, whenever you expend a use of your Channel Divinity, you can choose to give each ally within your aura Heroic Inspiration if it doesn’t already have it (no action required).

#### LEVEL 15: FORETOLD SMITE

When you cast Divine Smite, a weave of fate shows your allies a glimpse of who you truly are, and that you are meant for greatness. All allies within 60 feet of you gain the effects of the Bless spell until the start of your next turn (no Concentration required), and all enemies within 60 feet must make a Charisma saving throw against your spell save DC. On a failed save, a target suffers the effect of the Bane spell until the start of your next turn (no Concentration required).

#### LEVEL 20: FULFIL YOUR DESTINY

You gain 1 Destiny Point for your Power of Destiny feature whenever you finish a Long Rest. In addition, you can cast Word of Recall using this feature without expending a spell slot, but only to designate a sanctuary location. When you die, you are instead affected by the Resurrection spell and immediately have the teleportation effects of Word of Recall activate, targeting only you. Once you use this feature to designate a sanctuary location, you can’t use it again until you finish a Long Rest. However, when this feature revives you and activates the effect of Word of Recall, it will not function again until whatever caused you to die has been destroyed, killed, or otherwise defeated.

### Evil-Chaser (Ranger)

Relentlessly Pursue the Most Evil Evil-Chasers are most notably trained by the Interplanetary Control Force (ICF) to sniff out criminals and evil-doers, confront them, and incarcerate them. These Rangers are not all trained to serve in the ICF, as some Evil-Chasers come into their abilities naturally or through other means of seeing through their cunning schemes, clever disguises, and even magical ruses. When an Evil-Chaser gets the scent of its quarry, they just won’t give it up. GOOD-CHASERS? CHAOTIC-CHASERS? In the ThunderCats setting, this subclass represents a specific kind of Ranger that hunts down evil-doers. The terms and features the Evil-Chaser provides are bent toward targets with an Evil alignment, but players might wish to get permission from their GM to create a Good-Chaser, Neutral-Chaser, Chaotic-Chaser, or any other aspect of the alignment system that makes sense for their Ranger character. If that is the case, replace all references in the subclass for “evil” with whatever other alignment facet the character is a “-Chaser” of. This can be Lawful, Good, Neutral, or Chaotic.

#### LEVEL 3: EVIL-CHASER SPELLS

When you reach a Ranger level specified in the Evil-Chaser Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared. EVIL-CHASER SPELLS Ranger Level Spell 3 Command 5 Detect Thoughts 9 Clairvoyance 13 Banishment 17 Geas

#### LEVEL 3: EYE FOR EVIL

You can take a Study action to determine whether or not a target creature within line of sight has “Evil” as part of its alignment (DC 10 plus the target’s Deception or Performance modifier; whichever is higher). If you identify a creature’s alignment in this way, you have Advantage on Wisdom (Insight) checks made to discern its mood and intentions.

#### LEVEL 3: JUST YOU AND ME

When you cast Hunter’s Mark on a target of Evil or Unaligned alignment, it makes a Wisdom saving throw against your spell save DC. On a failed save, while Hunter’s Mark is affecting it, the creature has Disadvantage on attack rolls against any target other than you and can’t willingly move further than 30 feet away from you.

#### LEVEL 7: NO ESCAPE

When you cast Hunter’s Mark on a target of Evil or Unaligned alignment, the target must succeed on a Charisma saving throw against your spell save DC or have the Pacified condition while Hunter’s Mark is affecting it. In addition, creatures allied to the target within a 30-foot-radius Sphere centered on the target when Hunter’s Mark is cast must succeed on a Wisdom saving throw or have the Frightened condition. A Frightened target can repeat the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.

#### LEVEL 7: RIGHT TOOL FOR THE JOB

When you cast Hunter’s Mark on a target of Evil or Unaligned alignment, you may choose a different weapon mastery effect to apply to your attack rolls using a weapon when attacking that target. In addition, the extra damage from your Hunter’s Mark is increased by your Wisdom modifier (minimum 1) and can be any damage type you choose instead of Force damage.

#### LEVEL 11: PRIORITY TARGET

You gain a +3 bonus to Armor Class against attack rolls made by the target of your Hunter’s Mark. In addition, you have Advantage on all saving throws you make against effects originating from that target.

#### LEVEL 15: MOST WANTED

A target affected by your Hunter’s Mark has any Critical Hit it scores on you become a normal hit. You can cast Hunter’s Mark on a second target so long as it shares a creature type with the original target without losing Concentration. If you would otherwise lose Concentration, you choose which instance of Hunter’s Mark you lose Concentration on. In addition, you are considered to have Truesight in regards to viewing the targets of your Hunter’s Mark.

### Heckler (Rogue)

Sling Quips to Infuriate and Confound Not all Rogues need to use a dagger or a crossbow to hit a vital weak point on an enemy. The Heckler finds or makes a hole in the mental defenses of a foe and strikes at them with words that are as sharp or sharper than any weapon in their arsenal. Insults, mockery, and manipulating others’ self-esteem are the Heckler’s most vicious tools.

#### LEVEL 3: SHARP TONGUE

As a Bonus Action, you may goad an enemy within 30 feet of you that can understand your taunts. You take the Influence action as part of this action, the result of which sets the DC of a Charisma saving throw immediately made by the target. On a failed save, the target becomes heckled. You have Advantage on attack rolls against the heckled target, and it has Disadvantage on D20 Tests that are not targeting you or effects you create, such as ongoing spells. A target can cease being heckled in the following ways: • The target successfully inflicts damage on you. • The target rolls a 20 on the d20 during any D20 Test. • If the target is a Humanoid, it fails its saving throw against a Calm Emotions spell (it can’t willingly fail the saving throw). • The target spends 1 minute doing nothing but calming and re-centering themselves.

#### LEVEL 3: WORDS OF DISTRACTION

#### LEVEL 9: INFURIATING QUIP

A heckled target reduces its Passive Perception, Investigation and Perception checks, and Initiative rolls by a number equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum 1). As a Bonus Action, you can upgrade the detriments of a heckled target. You take the Influence action as part of this action, the result of which sets the DC of a Wisdom saving throw immediately made by the heckled target. On a failed save, the target falls into a disoriented rage while heckled. While in this rage, it has Disadvantage on Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma saving throws, and can’t cast or maintain spells that require Concentration.

#### LEVEL 13: THAT HAD TO HURT!

When a creature you can see fails a saving throw or suffers damage, you can take a Reaction to cause the target to become heckled. If the target is already heckled, it instead takes 4d6 Psychic damage. You use this feature a number of times equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum 1), and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### LEVEL 17: HAVING THE LAST WORD

Creatures now have Disadvantage on all saving throws made to avoid becoming heckled from your Sharp Tongue feature. In addition, you can add a number of Sneak Attack dice equal to your Proficiency Bonus when using your Sneak Attack feature against a heckled target. These extra Sneak Attack dice deal Psychic damage. You add these extra Sneak Attack dice a number of times equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum 1), and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

### Metamorphosis Sorcery

### (Sorcerer)

Command the Transformative Magics of Death’s Border There is a powerful release of magical, necromantic energy when something perishes. Those who command Metamorphosis Sorcery have the talents and knowledge needed to trap that energy through elaborate rites and rituals exclusive to their ilk. Preparing their mind through deep meditations and their bodies through self-mummification and canopic foci, Metamorphosis Sorcerers use those energies to transform into something wondrous, awesome, and arguably terrifying.

#### LEVEL 3: METAMORPHOSIS SPELLS

When you reach a Sorcerer level specified in the Metamorphosis Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared. METAMORPHOSIS SPELLS Sorcerer Level Spells 3 Alter Self, False Life, Inflict Wounds, Ray of Enfeeblement 5 Feign Death, Speak with Dead 7 Blight, Polymorph 9 Planar Binding, Raise Dead

#### LEVEL 3: MUMMIFICATION

The ritualistic mummification of your body provides you with Resistance to Necrotic and Poison damage. In addition, you learn the Chill Touch cantrip. A creature hit by your Chill Touch has the Weakened condition until the start of your next turn.

#### LEVEL 6: GRAVE CONNECTIONS

When you cast a spell that deals Necrotic damage, you can add your Charisma modifier to the damage roll (minimum 1). In addition, when you hit a creature with a spell that deals Necrotic damage, or it fails a saving throw against a spell you cast that deals Necrotic damage, it can be targeted by your spells with a range of Touch as long as it is within 120 feet and line of sight until the end of your next turn.

#### LEVEL 6: CANOPIC CURSES

You always have the Bestow Curse spell prepared. You can cast it a number of times without expending a spell slot equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses after finishing a Long Rest.

#### LEVEL 14:

#### OTHERWORLDLY STEP

You can vanish and reappear in a cloud of darkness and dust. When you are hit by an attack roll, you can take a Reaction to teleport up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space you can see. Each creature of your choice within 5 feet of the space you left and your destination space must succeed on an Intelligence saving throw against your spell save DC or have the Blinded condition until the end of your next turn. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### LEVEL 18: NECROMORPHOSIS

You have Immunity to Necrotic and Poison damage, as well as the Poisoned and Weakened conditions. In addition, as a Magic action, you can spend any number of Sorcery Points to endow yourself with the raw power of the grave, infusing your mummified living body with ancient blessings of the undying. For each Sorcery Point spent when activating this feature, you may choose one of the following benefits to apply for the next 10 minutes: • You gain 40 Temporary Hit Points. • You gain Advantage on attack rolls made as part of a Magic action. • Your melee attacks deal an extra 2d12 Necrotic damage. • You gain a bonus equal to your Proficiency Bonus to all saving throws. • You regain 2d4 spell levels’ worth of spell slots immediately. • You gain Truesight with a range of 90 feet. • You can add the Sap, Slow, or Vex (choose one) mastery property to spells with a range of Touch. • You can choose to be immune to the effects of Necromancy spells (no action required). • You add 4 to your Strength score and 2 to your Armor Class. • You gain Resistance to your choice of two damage types from Bludgeoning, Piercing, or Slashing. When the Necromorphosis ends, you have the Pacified condition until you finish a Short or Long Rest. Once you activate your Necromorphosis, you can’t do so again until you finish a Long Rest.

### Mongor Patron (Warlock)

Manipulate Power from Fear Itself MONGOR TRAITS D6 Effect 1 You grow huge claws that you can use to make an Unarmed Strike using your Strength or Charisma modifier (your choice). If you hit with this Unarmed Strike and deal damage, you can deal Slashing damage equal to 2d6 plus the modifier used for the attack roll, instead of the normal damage of an Unarmed Strike. If you make a melee spell attack by any means, you can make an Unarmed Strike with your claws as part of the same action. These claws wither and shatter after 10 minutes. 2 Your feet become large, furred, taloned paws that increase your walking Speed by 15 feet. The paws turn to ash, allowing your normal feet to emerge after 10 minutes. 3 A monstrous scythe appears in your hands which counts as a Martial weapon that you have proficiency with, that deals 2d6 Force damage on a hit. It has the Finesse and Reach properties, and the Graze mastery property, which you can use. The scythe crumbles to dust after 10 minutes. 4 You sprout long horns that you can use to make an Unarmed Strike using your Strength or Charisma modifier (your choice). If you hit with this Unarmed Strike and deal damage, you can deal Piercing damage equal to 1d12 plus the modifier used for the attack roll, instead of the normal damage of an Unarmed Strike. You have Advantage on the attack roll if you move 15+ feet in a straight line immediately before the attack. If you make a melee spell attack by any means, you can make an Unarmed Strike with your horns as part of the same action. The horns split and crack off after 10 minutes. 5 You become covered in dense, grey fur that gives you a base Armor Class 16 plus your Dexterity modifier. The fur grows mangy and falls off of you in clumps after 10 minutes. 6 You become wreathed in flames and gain Resistance to Fire damage. In addition, when you hit a creature within 5 feet of you with an attack roll, it deals an extra 1d10 Fire damage and the creature starts burning. The flames are snuffed out after 10 minutes. * Your eyes become the yellow orbs of Mongor, gaining Truesight out to 30 feet and Darkvision out to 120 feet. The orbs melt away like tears, reverting back to your normal eyes after 10 minutes. There are many reasons why a Warlock forges a pact with the demon lord of fear, Mongor. Perhaps they need an edge over their foes, have an intellectual curiosity about the nature of fear, or need to immerse themselves in terror to overcome their own insecurities. These Warlocks can summon powerful, fearsome energies that can penetrate the essence of the most emotionless of beings and grip their minds with terror.

#### LEVEL 3: MONGOR SPELLS

The magic of your pact ensures you always have certain spells ready; when you reach a Warlock level specified in the Mongor Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared. MONGOR SPELLS Warlock Level Spells 3 Bane, Dissonant Whispers, Hold Person, Mind Spike 5 Fear, Vampiric Touch 7 Confusion, Phantasmal Killer 9 Dream, Hold Monster

#### LEVEL 3: TERRORPHAGE

When you gain the Frightened condition, you gain Temporary Hit Points equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum 1) plus your Warlock level. While you have these Temporary Hit Points, you have Resistance to Psychic damage. In addition, as a Bonus Action while Frightened, you can ignore the effects of the condition until the start of your next turn.

#### LEVEL 3: VILLAINOUS LAUGHTER

Once during your turn, when you hit a creature with an attack roll, you can laugh maniacally and cause it to succeed on a Wisdom saving throw against your spell save DC or have the Frightened condition until the end of your next turn. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Short or Long Rest.

#### LEVEL 6: ASPECT OF THE MONGOR

As a Bonus Action, you can channel the fiendish aspects of the demon lord Mongor. Roll twice on the Mongor Traits table below and take either result to determine the effects of the feature. If both rolls are the same result, you can choose that result’s effect, or choose the final effect on the table. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### LEVEL 6: EMBODY THEIR FEAR

#### LEVEL 14: AVATAR OF THE MONGOR

When a creature fails a saving throw against one of your Warlock spells, you can give yourself the Frightened condition until you lose the Temporary Hit Points gained from your Terrorphage feature (no action required). You ignore the effects of the Frightened condition when you gain it in this way. Once you use this feature, you can’t use it again until you finish a Short or Long Rest. As a Magic action, your body shape-shifts into an avatar of Mongor. You gain the following benefits for 1 minute: Aspects of Mongor. You gain all of the effects from your Aspect of the Mongor feature. Damage Resistance. You have Resistance to all types of damage except Radiant. Flaming Breath. You can cast Dragon’s Breath (Fire damage only) once, requiring no Somatic or Material components at your highest Pact Magic slot level, without expending a spell slot.

#### LEVEL 10: PIERCING HORROR

Your attack rolls deal an extra 1d4 Psychic damage. This damage is increased to 1d8 if the target has the Frightened condition.

### Magician (Wizard)

Perform Feats of Awe Alongside Spellcraft The Magician commands and weaves the same magical energies as other Wizards, but does so with the flair and circumstance of a performer. They see the world in different dimensions and calculations, manipulating their local reality with baffling degrees of poise and misdirection. With a skilled Magician, there is no telling where a simple trick ends and their spells begin.

#### LEVEL 3: APPRENTICE BASICS

You learn the Mage Hand, Prestidigitation, and Thaumaturgy cantrips. If you already know one of these cantrips, you learn a different Wizard cantrip of your choice for each cantrip from this feature you already know.

#### LEVEL 3: LEGERDEMAIN

Your Mage Hand has a range of 60 feet, can manifest as any texture or color, and can even manifest as Invisible. You can change any aspect of the hand’s appearance as a Bonus Action. In addition, when casting a Wizard spell, you can make a Dexterity (Sleight of Hand) check to hide the spell’s components (no action required). Any creature with a Passive Perception that is less than the result doesn’t realize you are casting a spell.

#### LEVEL 6: PRACTICED ADJUSTMENTS

Anytime you change your prepared Wizard spells, you may choose a number of them equal to your Intelligence modifier (minimum 1) plus your Proficiency Bonus. When casting the chosen spells, you can ignore any one component (Verbal, Somatic, or Material) needed to cast the spell. If the spell’s Material component has a listed GP cost, that Material component can’t be ignored.

#### LEVEL 10: NOW YOU SEE IT…

As long as you do not move on your turn, you can take a Bonus Action to open two linked magical portals that only you (and creatures with Truesight) can see. Choose two separate 5-foot spaces you can see within 30 feet of you. A portal that fills that space emerges in each of those spaces, lasting until you end your turn more than 30 feet from either portal or your Concentration ends. Despite concentrating on the portals, you can additionally cast other spells requiring Concentration. If you lose Concentration while having cast such a spell, you lose Concentration on both the portals and the spell. While the portals are open, you treat the spaces where the portals exist as if they were the same space. You treat anything adjacent to one portal space as adjacent to the other portal space. You can also look through one portal space as if you are looking from the other portal space. In addition, casting a spell with a range of 10 feet or greater to one portal space can then extend from the other portal space (in any direction) as if the distance between the two didn’t exist.
