The entrance is buried beneath the roots of the tree, its heavy stone door sealed by ancient magic designed to contain Grune’s vengeful spirit. ADVENTURE HOOK The original purpose of Grune’s Tomb has faded into legend, leaving most inhabitants of Third Earth unaware of its existence. Travelers who wander too near may break the ancient seal by accident, awakening the terror that is Grune the Destroyer from his long burial beneath the earth. ADVENTURE HOOK Far across the stars, the planet Blue Plunder is artificially powered by a common rock found under Third Earth’s sea. Seeking a solution for their energy shortage, the planet’s leaders sent a salvage expert, a cold-hearted

#### HILLS OF ELFSHIMA

Beyond the Onyx Pyramid lies the rolling range known as the Hills of Elfshima. At dawn, their dark silhouettes seem to form the shape of a slumbering body stretched across the horizon. ADVENTURE HOOK In reality, the hills are not hills at all, but the petrified form of the Child of Gorgon, a colossal being turned to stone in ages past. The name “Elfshima” conceals a clue to its secret, for it is an anagram of “I am flesh.” With the powerful artifact known as the Mask of Gorgon, the Child can be restored to life. When the Mask is imbued with the power of “Sight Beyond Sight,” its gaze can awaken the stone giant from its eternal slumber. This artifact rests in the Treetop Kingdom, guarded by the warrior maidens.

#### HOOK MOUNTAIN

Hook Mountain looms among the jagged peaks of Third Earth, its summit curving downward like a colossal hook reaching down from the frozen sky. Constant blizzards batter its slopes, and avalanches thunder through its ravines. The harsh weather and very low temperatures do not deter the snowfolk, however, who live in a castle at the mountain’s summit. ADVENTURE HOOK Beneath the ice and rock lies an abandoned thundrillium mine, long rumored to be cursed. It is said that anyone who enters it never comes out again. None know that the true reason for this is the Mad Bubbler. This ancient phantom exudes shimmering bubbles filled with a mind-twisting gas. Those who inhale it succumb to “mine madness,” a delirious fury that awakens the darkest corners of their hearts.

#### KINGDOM OF WEBS

The Kingdom of Webs is a ruins-filled giant cave located under the Forest of Giant Insects. It is the home of Spidera and her numerous spider minions. This web-filled, spider-infested dungeon spells certain doom for anybody who enters it. ADVENTURE HOOK Taking the form of a glittering diamondfly fairie, the devil-priest Mumm-Ra plans to lure unsuspecting victims into the Forest of Giant Insects. Using a hypnotic, dazzling light, he will lead them toward the hidden entrances of Spidera’s lair, where the spider minions lie in wait to ensnare prey and drag them deep into the web-filled caverns below.

#### MAFTET’S TEMPLE

Hidden beneath the sands lies Maftet’s Temple, the long-forgotten home of Maftet, the lynx-god. The temple is an immense underground chamber located directly below a set of ancient ruins in the Desert of Sinking Sands, not far from Mumm-Ra’s Onyx Pyramid. One of its buried passages even connects to the River of Doom, which flows all the way beneath Mumm-Ra’s lair. The vast chamber glitters with hoarded gold, jewels, and relics of untold worth. Maftet dwells here alone, bound by duty to guard his riches. Over centuries of solitude, his devotion has turned to obsession. Forgotten by worshipers, the once-proud god has become frail and sorrowful. ADVENTURE HOOK Maftet’s Temple has remained sealed for countless years, its secrets buried beneath the sands. It is said that one of its tunnels offers a hidden route to Mumm-Ra’s lair, though any who seek it must first face Maftet himself. The temple’s treasures are vast as well, yet those who covet them risk Maftet’s wrath. Although his body is failing, Maftet retains a fragment of divine strength. Intruders who aid him when he stumbles, rather than attacking, may earn his trust and friendship. However, touching his treasure without permission instantly breaks that fragile bond, and the god’s fury will fall upon the greedy.

#### MAZE OF INFINITY

The Maze of Infinity is a sprawling network of interconnected caves and tunnels. With only one entrance and one exit, its twisting passages make it nearly impossible to navigate without careful planning or luck. Alongside winding corridors, the Maze contains underwater streams of thick, putty-like water. ADVENTURE HOOK The Maze is home to the reclusive under-earthmen. Centuries ago, their ancestors were driven underground by the tyrannical “over-earthmen”. The under-earthmen have preserved their most precious books, yet generations of living without light have left their eyesight weakened. Unable to read their own texts, they capture intruders, hoping these “descendants of the over-earthmen” can read aloud from their treasured books.

#### MICRITS’ TERRITORY

Just beyond Cats’ Lair lies a stretch of gentle grasslands known as the Micrits’ Territory. Here lives a diminutive race of people standing no taller than an inch. The micrits make their homes in the tall grass at forest edges, building tiny huts of bark and straw. Their society is agrarian, cultivating mushrooms and small berries that thrive in the shade of the taller grasses. Micrit technology is of the simple and practical type, with fire their most advanced form of energy manipulation. However, they employ a web-thin fiber known as glow strands, composed in part of Thundranium. These near-invisible threads are both flexible and incredibly strong, and the micrits can telekinetically manipulate them to ensnare or release objects at a distance. ADVENTURE HOOK The micrits’ small stature and proximity to Cats’ Lair make them vulnerable to accidental destruction. A single careless step can flatten homes, farms, or entire families. When such a tragedy occurs, the micrits vow revenge upon the “giants” who brought ruin to their village. Under cover of night they exact their vengeance, sabotaging vehicles and machinery, stealing weapons, and binding any sleeping foes in their nearly invisible glow strands.

#### MIDNIGHT WOODS

The Midnight Woods is a dense and tangled forest where sunlight rarely reaches the ground. The thick canopy of trees and overgrown vegetation shroud the area in perpetual twilight, giving the woods their ominous name. Few dare to enter, for the darkness plays tricks on the eyes and the mind alike. ADVENTURE HOOK Ever the deceiver, Mumm-Ra uses the Midnight Woods to turn his enemies against one another. Taking advantage of the poor visibility, he disguises himself as a worried traveler and sends them into the forest separately. Each is warned of a “Shadow Robber” who preys upon those who pass through. Once inside, the victims mistake each other for the robber, falling into conflict while Mumm-Ra watches their chaos unfold.

#### MINES OF THE MOLEFOLK

Beneath the Pits of the Molefolk lies a sprawling network of tunnels and caverns. These mines are home to the gentle, soft-spoken molefolk, who toil endlessly under the command of their cruel overseer, Molemaster. The Molemaster’s greed for gold has twisted him into a despot who forces his kin to dig ever deeper into the earth. ADVENTURE HOOK The constant digging of the molefolk often causes tremors and collapses that damage nearby settlements or structures. Neighboring realms see this as an act of aggression, unaware of the tyranny beneath their feet. Should Molemaster be overthrown, the molefolk will cease their harmful digging and seek peaceful relations with those above.

#### MOUNT ANGUISH

Mount Anguish is a towering peak marked by haunting ruins and grim carvings. One side of the mountain bears the likeness of a demonoid face, its jagged cliffs forming fangs and hollow eyes that seem to watch the valley below. At its summit stands a ring of massive stone slabs arranged in a circular pattern, the remnants of an ancient henge that once served as a place of great power. ADVENTURE HOOK Mount Anguish serves as a site of dark rituals for Mumm-Ra. Each ceremony demands rare, specific reagents, and gathering them risks drawing attention to the dark magic unfolding atop the mountain. Thus Mumm-Ra has plenty of magical traps awaiting any interlopers.

#### ONYX PYRAMID

Rising from the Desert of Sinking Sands, the Onyx Pyramid has served as Mumm-Ra’s home on Third Earth for centuries. Its central pyramid is surrounded by four towering obelisks, which focus energy during powerful conjuring events and shoot lightning at intruders. It stands atop a crossroads of pure energies deep within the earth, a foundation that amplifies both its power and its peril. Despite its current evil, the Onyx Pyramid was once aligned with forces of good, designed by the ancient Egyptians to entomb Mumm-Ra forever. The Pyramid has three primary entrances; one at ground level that Mumm-Ra can reveal at will, another near the top is primarily used when he assumes his Ever-Living form, and a third leads into the basement from the underground River of Doom. Other secret entrances are said to exist as well, hidden and nearly impossible to find without extraordinary cunning and relentless investigation. Inside, the Pyramid contains countless secret chambers, dark creatures, and forbidden mysteries known only to Mumm-Ra. The most visible is the main chamber, which holds his sarcophagus, magic cauldron, and the statues of the Ancient Spirits of Evil, from which he draws his formidable power. The Pyramid’s passages are patrolled by foul beings conjured by Mumm-Ra. Giant lightning-spewing lizards, harpies, enormous snakes, and the undead remains of those lost in the Sinking Sands all serve to protect the dark secrets within. ADVENTURE HOOK Mumm-Ra is always concocting evil schemes from within the Onyx Pyramid, and it is where he is strongest. Assaulting it is a perilous task, but sometimes necessary to foil his plans. The Pyramid’s twisting, labyrinthine halls confound intruders, concealing artifacts of immense power, deadly traps, and passages that open only at the will of the Ever-Living. Even those who survive the maze must still face Mumm-Ra himself, a monumental challenge after enduring the full gauntlet of his lair.

#### OPAL PYRAMID

The Opal Pyramid is the home of the good sorceress MummRana. Just as Mumm-Rana is the polar opposite of Mumm-Ra, so is her pyramid the complete opposite of his. Its shining white walls stand in contrast to the dark stone of Mumm-Ra’s Onyx Pyramid. Also similarly to the Onyx Pyramid, within its halls stand four statues representing the Ancient Spirits of Good, and a cauldron filled with magical waters. Where Mumm-Ra keeps a sarcophagus, Mumm-Rana instead rests upon a simple stone slab where she renews her strength. ADVENTURE HOOK Mumm-Rana has long struggled against the rampant evil of Third Earth and spends most of her time slowly restoring her strength. She remains largely unaware of Mumm-Ra and his connection to the Ancient Spirits of Evil. Should the devil-priest choose to strike, she would stand little chance against him. Mumm-Ra could overpower her faltering power and twist it against the ThunderCats, forcing them to face a being of pure goodness they cannot bring themselves to destroy.

#### PHOSPHORUS DESERT

The Phosphorus Desert is a vast, barren expanse, one of the many deserts of Third Earth. Its sands are infamous for igniting at the slightest friction, earning it the name “burning sands.” Traveling through this wasteland is treacherous, as every step risks setting the ground ablaze. Only those who move quickly and carefully stand a chance of crossing it unscathed. ADVENTURE HOOK For those with access to speed, the Phosphorus Desert is the safest land route to Mount Anguish, a site of Mumm-Ra’s dark rituals. Its treacherous terrain also makes it an ideal hiding place for secrets that must remain beyond the reach of ordinary travelers, accessible only by magic or those who can fly.

#### PIT OF THE NETHERWITCH

The Pit of the Netherwitch is a foreboding cave, its entrance shaped like a gaping maw lined with jagged fangs. Reaching it requires crossing the Bridge of Slime, a slick, treacherous span over the River of Despair. Once inside the cave, there is a steep drop into the actual pit. The darkness within the pit conceals a number of strange and terrifying creatures. The main resident of the pit is the Netherwitch, a hag who is purported to have many magical powers, including the power to transport any being to other planes. ADVENTURE HOOK The Netherwitch isn’t the most pleasant creature to deal with. She has grown particularly nasty from spending years within her pit. She seeks a broomstick to give her freedom and a witch’s cat. A Thunderian would also suit her nicely as a combination witch’s cat and apprentice. Mumm-Ra also likes to take advantage of the Netherwitch’s reputation. He assumes a far more pleasing form to enemies of his who seek her out, using his own powers to imitate the Netherwitch’s abilities until it’s too late for his victims to realize it’s him.

#### PLUN-DARR COVE

Plun-Darr Cove is a small, sheltered bay along the shoreline near Castle Plun-Darr. Its calm waters provide a safe harbor for ships, making it an ideal anchorage for the cyborg pirate Berserkers. From this hidden inlet, they launch raids and slip unnoticed into the inland waterways. ADVENTURE HOOK Plun-Darr Cove serves as the main base for Berserker raiders who prey on the nearby fishing villages. Any captives are held aboard the ships moored here, awaiting ransom or worse. The local fisherfolk are peaceful to a fault and refuse to take violent action, leaving them vulnerable. Heroes who are convinced to help them will have to navigate the villagers’ reluctance while confronting the dangerous, relentless pirates that threaten the coast.

#### RAVAGE ISLAND

#### RIVER OF DOOM

Ravage Island is a small, rocky, barren and uninhabited islet. Its cliffs rise sharply from the sea, and the jagged outcroppings make landing difficult. At the center of the island stands a ruined glass dome housing a massive high-tech beacon, a relic from a previous incarnation of Third Earth. Its original purpose has been lost to history, and almost no one living remembers its true function. The River of Doom is a treacherous river that flows beneath Mumm-Ra’s Pyramid. Its source is unknown, but it flows past Maftet’s Temple and continues all the way to the tomb chamber deep beneath Mumm-Ra’s lair. The river is dangerous at every turn. Its waters rush with deadly force, and the current is home to bizarre and malevolent creatures. Since the river runs directly beneath MummRa’s Pyramid, the devil-priest exerts complete control over it, able to summon any manner of horrors to defend his domain. ADVENTURE HOOK When special hypnotic crystals are placed inside the beacon, it emits mesmeric rays capable of reaching great distances and bending the minds of anyone who looks upon them. The effect also draws victims toward Ravage Island, where the beacon’s power is amplified. Mumm-Ra, one of the few who understands the beacon’s purpose, has enhanced the crystals with his magic. He’s also equipped mu’tants with protective goggles to shield them from the effects. With the beacon active, Mumm-Ra plans to trap the ThunderCats in manifestations of their own minds, turning their greatest fears and memories against them. ADVENTURE HOOK A mysterious boatman navigates the River of Doom in a small wooden vessel with a single oar. He offers passage for a price, but he secretly serves Mumm-Ra. Any traveler who opposes the devil-priest may find themselves trapped and attacked while isolated on the river. Other threats lurk as well, including the Incubi, which hang cocooned from the river ceiling and awaken at Mumm-Ra’s command. The tunnels themselves can come alive, and a thick, choking fog can be summoned to disorient intruders.

#### RIVER OF DESPAIR

#### RIVER OF POISON

The River of Despair is the largest and longest river on Third Earth. It begins in the tuska’s homeland of Tuskania and winds through numerous regions, providing a vital water source for the settlements along its banks. Despite its importance, the river is filled with dangers, from unpredictable currents to monstrous creatures. The River of Poison winds through a dense, shadowed forest, flowing into Dismal Falls before draining into the Swamp of Serpents. Its waters are a sickly greenish-yellow and carry a potent toxin, giving the river its infamous name. A thick, lethal mist clings to the banks, reducing visibility to near nothing. The region is dark and foreboding, and even the bravest ThunderCats venture here only in their Thermal Catsuits, braving the deadly environment with extreme caution. ADVENTURE HOOK The River of Despair is essential to the surrounding civilizations, but its constant threats make travel perilous. Lightningfast Giant Eels emerge from hidden caves, carnivorous Fish-Eating Flowers swarm the riverbed, and haunting Vampire Mermaids sing sailors to their doom. Travelers must also beware the notorious Black Widow Shark and the Tongue-a-Saurus, striking without warning, ensuring that no journey along the river is ever safe. ADVENTURE HOOK The River of Poison is no place for the unprepared. Drinking even a small amount of its waters, even accidentally, can induce a deadly illness. Only powerful magic can save those who fall victim to the river or the poisonous Swamp of Serpents that it feeds.

#### ROCKS END

Rocks End is a small, rugged region marked by jagged outcrops and hidden caves. Amid the stone and shadow grow brackenberries, beautiful but deadly, and a few rare plants that secrete a liquid capable of neutralizing their poison. A gentle, cat-like creature named Kudi makes her home in one of the caves, offering aid to those in need. ADVENTURE HOOK When weary travelers take shelter among the caves of Rocks End, they may mistake the tempting brackenberries for safe food. Should they fall victim to the poison, Kudi will emerge to save them with her curative draught. Ever seeking an opportunity for deception, however, Mumm-Ra waits for the moment of gratitude, appearing in Kudi’s stead, transformed and ready to worm his way into the travelers’ ranks.

#### RUINS OF DARK SIDE

Deep within the forbidden lands of Dark Side lie the crumbling remains of an ancient temple. Within its echoing halls rest the legendary Sound Stones, artifacts said to hold immense sonic power. Guarding them is Sondora, a devoted priestess sworn to protect the stones from those who would misuse their power. At the temple’s entrance sit the Guardians of the sound stones, stone gargoyles that awaken the instant anyone attempts to steal from the temple’s sacred vaults. The stones themselves rest in an ornate chest, pulsing with a high-pitched resonance that can harm intruders. ADVENTURE HOOK The mu’tants have uncovered the temple’s location from an ancient map and, with aid from the Lunataks, have stolen one of the sound stones. They now use its resonance to power a devastating sonic gun.

#### SKY-TOMB

After the Lunataks were released by the mu’tants under the orders of Mumm-Ra, the first thing they needed was a headquarters, so they embarked on constructing Sky-Tomb. Sky-Tomb is the flying fortress and residence of the evil Lunataks. The fortress is completely mobile, and is capable of flying to any location on Third Earth. The fortress,very powerful and technologically highly advanced, is fueled by thundrillium.

ADVENTURE HOOK The Lunataks forced brutefolk, wollos, and bolkins to labor on Sky-Tomb’s construction, and many captives remain imprisoned within its walls as bait for would-be heroes. Any attempt to free them or disrupt the Lunataks’ plans requires infiltrating the fortress itself. Sky-Tomb is the core of their operations, making a direct assault incredibly dangerous. Its mobility complicates any attack, but the fortress becomes vulnerable when it stops to refill its water stores, offering a rare opportunity to board through its ventilation shafts.

#### SWAMP OF SERPENTS

The Swamp of Serpents is a bleak expanse of marshland where life struggles to survive. Thick fog blankets the area, carrying the stench of decay and the sting of poison. The swamp’s waters are lethally toxic, their surface rippling with a sickly green sheen. The River of Poison feeds this mire, plunging over Dismal Falls before spreading into the stagnant wetlands below. Even the brave ThunderCats rarely venture here, and when they do, they rely on their Thermal Catsuits to withstand the swamp’s hostile conditions. ADVENTURE HOOK Beneath the choking mists, colossal serpents prowl the waters, their scales slick with venom and their eyes glowing a deep crimson. These predators are swift, vicious, immune to the swamp’s toxins, and capable of striking down anything that flies too close. The mu’tants have turned this deadly landscape into a weapon, luring their enemies into the swamp in hopes that the serpents will strike or that their prey will stumble into the poisonous waters. It is a risky tactic, but one they are more than willing to gamble.

#### TOWER OF OMENS

The Tower of Omens is the second home built by the ThunderCats on Third Earth, constructed alongside the berbils. As new Thunderian refugees arrived and threats from the Lunataks grew, the ThunderCats raised this tower to watch over their lands. Perched at the edge of the Forest of Mists, the tower overlooks the only safe passage into Dark Side that the ThunderCats can traverse. From its highest point, a powerful scanner sweeps the horizon, keeping watch for any signs of Lunatak activity. ADVENTURE HOOK The Tower of Omens stands as the first line of defense against Lunatak incursions. Strange movements in Dark Side, mysterious energy readings, or sudden attacks often send the ThunderCats racing to the tower to investigate, defend, or uncover what the Lunataks are planning.

#### TREETOP KINGDOM

High within the dense forests of Third Earth lies the Treetop Kingdom, a sprawling network of homes woven among the branches. This arboreal realm is inhabited by the Warrior Maidens, a tight-knit community of fierce and skilled archers who are deeply territorial and intolerant of intruders. Their deep knowledge of the forest, from its creatures to its plants, allows them to navigate and defend it with unmatched precision. While they rely primarily on traditional weapons, they sometimes use hot air balloons to gain an aerial advantage when needed.

When the ThunderCats and the mu’tants first arrived on Third Earth, the Warrior Maidens were cautious, viewing both groups as potential threats. Over time, however, they became trusted allies of the ThunderCats. The Maidens have aided the ThunderCats on many occasions, and vice versa, with the ThunderCats even providing an early warning system to detect approaching mu’tants. Unlike other allies such as the berbils, who depend heavily on ThunderCats protection, the Warrior Maidens are fiercely independent and prefer to handle challenges on their own, calling on the ThunderCats only in the most dire circumstances. The Warrior Maidens are led by Willa, who often represents them in dealings with outsiders, including the ThunderCats. Her younger sister, Nayda, serves as second-in-command and chief scout. ADVENTURE HOOK

#### TUSKANIA

Technopede, a sentient war machine shaped like a massive mechanical centipede. Eight powerful legs propel it across the land, and a heavy armored tail lashes out at anything in its path. Its head bristles with laser cannons, and it can launch explosive cluster bombs that detonate on impact. The Technopede relentlessly attacks Tuskania, targeting both the tuskas and the River of Despair. Despite their bravery and advanced weaponry, the tuskas struggle to stop it. In desperation, Turmagar seeks skilled outsiders to confront the creature and protect his homeland. Even weapons made of thundrillium have little effect on the Technopede, making this a challenge only for the most daring heroes. ADVENTURE HOOK Shemlock is the tallest and oldest tree in the Treetop Kingdom, towering above all others in the forest. Embedded within its trunk is the Mask of Gorgon, guarded by the Warrior Maidens. Mumm-Ra desires the Mask and plans to use the Lunataks, with their advanced technology, to overpower the Maidens. If he succeeds, he intends to retrieve the Mask and awaken the colossal Child of Gorgon in the Hills of Elfshima, bringing devastation to all of Third Earth.

#### UNICORN FOREST

to the noble Unicorns and the devoted Unicorn Keepers. Although the Keepers work tirelessly to protect these magical creatures, the forest is filled with dangers both natural and magical. TUSKANIA Tuskania is the homeland of Turmagar and his people, the tuskas, anthropomorphic walrus folk. The land consists of tight-knit settlements clustered along the River of Despair, the lifeblood of Tuskania. The tuskas rely heavily on the river’s waters for survival and are fiercely protective of it. Under the leadership of Turmagar, the tuska are courageous and willing to confront any threat head-on. Armed with specialized guns capable of firing explosive charges, they are formidable in battle. Yet outside of combat, the tuskas are gentle and friendly, devoted to their families and community. UNICORN FOREST ADVENTURE HOOK The trees of Unicorn Forest are enchanted, but not all enchantments are benign. Dark magic can twist the trees into monstrous forms that prey on the Unicorns. On top of this, careless or malicious groups, such as cyborg pirate Berserkers and mu’tants, raid the forest to capture unicorns for food, spell reagents, or evil schemes. The Unicorn Keepers are vigilant, but the forest is vast and they welcome assistance to help defend its creatures.

#### VALLEY OF CHAINS

On the outskirts of Dark Side lies the Valley of Chains, a bleak region scattered with the ruins of a long-forgotten civilization. Legends speak of a curse laid by an ancient wizard, whose vengeance still lingers across the desolate landscape. At first glance, the valley appears barren and harmless, but its true danger lies beneath the surface. Hidden underground are living chains that awaken when travelers attempt to cross, erupting from the earth to ensnare and drag victims below. ADVENTURE HOOK The Lunataks often float their flying fortress, Sky-Tomb, above the Valley of Chains to strengthen their defenses. When the fortress hovers in place, all routes in or out pass directly through the haunted valley, forcing anyone who dares approach to face the animated chains. Only when Sky-Tomb relocates is there a fleeting chance to enter or escape.

#### VORTEX

Beneath the looming cliffs of Hook Mountain lies the Vortex, a strange and dangerous phenomenon. A small, rocky outcrop juts from the mountainside, its gaping, jagged, mouth-shaped cave containing a sentient windstorm that endlessly inhales and exhales through its cavernous maw, stirring the mountain’s air into deadly tempests. There is no ledge before the cave’s entrance, only a sheer drop into the mountain’s face. Fierce downdrafts sweep down from the peaks, dragging the unwary into chasms and, sometimes, straight into the Vortex itself. Those who enter may find themselves lost within its timeless currents, spinning through moments both past and future. The only thing within the cave is a single tree that sways in the ceaseless gale, offering a sliver of hope to anyone trapped within the living whirlwind’s grasp. ADVENTURE HOOK Travelers who stray too close to Hook Mountain’s chasms, or to the gaping mouth of its cave, risk being pulled in by the Vortex’s vacuum. Enemies such as the mu’tants sometimes make use of this danger, using voice imitators to mimic cries for help and lure would-be rescuers into the storm. Once drawn in, they must confront the storm’s will itself to escape its endless, howling depths.

#### WHIRLPOOL OF INFINITY

The Whirlpool of Infinity is a massive, churning vortex in the River of Despair. Its waters spin with incredible speed, creating a violent current that drags in anything nearby. Many have been swept away by the whirlpool, making it one of the deadliest hazards along the river. ADVENTURE HOOK Those who fall into the River of Despair, and aren’t immediately ripped to pieces by its many denizens, are swept by the current into the Whirlpool of Infinity, where escape seems impossible. The whirlpool can also serve as a convenient, but dangerous, place to hide or dispose of objects of great importance.

#### WOLLO VILLAGE

The Wollo Village is a humble settlement of cement homes reinforced with wood and scrap metal. It is inhabited by the gentle wollos, a peaceful people who live simple, contented lives far removed from the wars and power struggles that trouble much of Third Earth. Most make their living as farmers and fishermen, tending small fields and casting nets along the coast where a few weathered boats rest at anchor. With little access to advanced technology, the wollos rely on handmade tools and sturdy donkeys for their daily labor and travel. Life in Wollo Village changed with the arrival of the ThunderCats and the mu’tants. The once-quiet community now finds itself caught between these two alien forces, often suffering in the wake of their battles. The mu’tants take cruel pleasure in raiding the defenseless villagers, destroying homes and seizing supplies for sport. Yet whenever danger looms, the ThunderCats rush to their defense, earning the villagers’ loyalty and gratitude. Still, whispers have begun to spread about whether these conflicts would have ever reached the Wollo Village at all if the ThunderCats had never come. ADVENTURE HOOK Though far removed from the greater conflicts of Third Earth, the wollos’ kindness and trust have made them easy targets for those with darker intentions. Recently, a band of pig-like tabbuts has begun extorting the villagers, demanding rent and supplies in exchange for so-called “protection.” To make matters worse, the rocky mountain that looms beyond the village has begun to grow unstable. Its crumbling slopes threaten to unleash a devastating landslide should an earthquake strike, one that could bury the peaceful village unless help arrives in time.

### New Thundera

New Thundera is the result of the reformation of the destroyed planet Thundera. While the ThunderCats and many other inhabitants escaped before the planet’s destruction, some flora and fauna survived on the larger fragments of its shattered surface. Over the years, these fragments gradually gathered in a single region, allowing the planet to miraculously reform. This process created a world that blends the familiar with the new. Many of Thundera’s original buildings, landmarks, and inhabitants remain, while new creatures and environments emerged during the planet’s reconstruction. Unlike its predecessor, New Thundera formed very close to the Moons of Plun-Darr, which now orbit it. These Moons have a strong influence on some areas of the planet which are closest to them.

### Geography

New Thundera is a solemn ruin of once-beautiful environments now scarred by the original destruction and tainted by the influence of the Moons of Plun-Darr. Traces of its former splendor remain, though they are but faint echoes of the Thundera that once was. The glimmering domes and minarets of Thunderian civilization have collapsed into sheared metal, crumbling stone, and scattered rubble. Only a few small villages remain, yet without inhabitants they have become little more than ghost towns.

### History

After Jaga seized the Sword of Plun-Darr from Ratilla the Terrible, he cast it into the deepest canyons of Thundera, hoping to seal its evil power forever. Unbeknownst to him, the sword’s immense energy destabilized the planet itself, causing Thundera to explode. While the ThunderCats and several other inhabitants escaped before the catastrophe, many didn’t. However, some managed to survive on the massive fragments left drifting in space. One of these survivors was the ancient Thunderian sorceress Jagara. Using her creation, the Mighty Gyroscope, Jagara stabilized the remnants of Thundera and generated a new gravitational core. This device drew together the fragments of Thundera, not only binding them together but also reforming them into the renewed world of New Thundera. The newly formed planet still retained a number of buildings, inhabitants, and geographical locations of Thundera, as well as new creatures and environments that were formed with the planet. Unlike its predecessor, New Thundera reformed very close to the Moons of Plun-Darr. The Mighty Gyroscope’s powerful gravity inadvertently pulled these moons into orbit, bringing their corrupting influence to certain areas of the planet. It was Mumm-Ra, the devil-priest, who first discovered the planet’s return. Traveling across time and space from Third Earth, he sought both the Sword of Plun-Darr and the Treasure of Thundera, a collection of powerful artifacts tied to Thundera’s ancient legacy. Enslaving the snarfs, he constructed a new onyx pyramid to aid his evil search. Mumm-Ra eventually recovered the Sword of Plun-Darr, but the ThunderCats freed the snarfs, destroyed his pyramid, and pursued him relentlessly. In a cunning ploy, Mumm-Ra tricked them into uncovering the Treasure of Thundera, only to try stealing it for himself. However, as he fled, the Sword of Omens struck him, shattering the chest that contained the Treasure and scattering its pieces across New Thundera. Defeated, Mumm-Ra retreated to his pyramid on Third Earth, while the ThunderCats, determined to preserve their legacy, also returned there to prepare for the arduous task of reclaiming each lost artifact before he could.

### Points of Interest

Much of old Thundera lies in ruin or tainted by corruption, giving rise to strange new lands across New Thundera. While the planet holds many continents and scattered islands, the lands most significant to the ThunderCats’ adventures in New Thundera are detailed below in alphabetical order.

#### BALEFUL SWAMP

Once a radiant lake crossed by the glittering Sacred Barge, Baleful Swamp is now a grotesque echo of its former beauty. The destruction and rebirth of the world have warped every living thing here, none more terrifying than the gargantuan snapping turtle at its heart. It lurks beneath the murky waters, its shell choked with moss and roots until it resembles a small island. The beast waits in perfect stillness for prey to step onto its back, then snaps around with sudden violence, its jaws capable of crushing bone in a single strike. ADVENTURE HOOK According to the Book of Omens, the Baleful Swamp is one of the sacred ritual sites required to restore New Thundera to its former splendor and avert its second destruction from the spreading corruption of the Moons of Plun-Darr. The Golden Oar from the Treasure of Thundera must be brought to the Baleful Swamp and placed into the swamp water at the twenty-fourth hour of the ritual. Only then can New Thundera be saved.

#### CANYONS OF YOUTH

From a distance, the Canyons of Youth appears serene and inviting, with vibrant trees, colorful rainbows, and sparkling lakes. It sits adjacent to the Caverns of Cold, making it easy to spot from that icy region. At first glance, it seems like a perfect respite from the frost, but there is a hidden danger. This magical canyon has the power to reverse the age of anyone who steps inside. Visitors often feel a sudden euphoria that draws them closer. The moment they cross the threshold, they transform into children and lose all memory of their adult

#### CAVERNS OF COLD

The Caverns of Cold stretch across a rocky, frostbitten region where temperatures drop so low that entering unprepared could lead to serious injury or death. This frigid climate is amplified by the influence of Plun-Darr’s second moon, whose icy aura seeps into the tunnels. Even with specialized warmth, enough to break the frost, the ice-coated tunnels still pose major risks. Floors are slick, and hidden crevasses can swallow the unwary. One wrong step could send a traveler plunging into darkness before anyone notices. The caverns are lives. The Canyons of Youth can appear as a fairytale paradise, but whether it is a blessing or a curse depends entirely on perspective. ADVENTURE HOOK Travelers forced to leave the Caverns of Cold due to freezing conditions may stumble into the Canyons of Youth while seeking shelter. The canyon is also known to attract the elderly or those seeking a second chance at youth. However, this rejuvenation is not permanent and can be reversed by powerful magic, leaving opportunities for both rescue and intrigue. also dangerously close to other hazardous areas such as the Canyons of Youth, meaning a single misstep could lead from one danger straight into another. ADVENTURE HOOK Mumm-Ra has created a batch of freezon crystals using arctic salt and thundercatnip. He has dispatched Ma-Mutt to place them deep within the Caverns of Cold. The potent thundercatnip fumes irresistibly lure Thunderians to the crystals, freezing them instantly upon contact.

#### CHURNING ROCKS

Churning Rocks, also called the Churning Canyon, is among New Thundera’s most dangerous locations. Towering stone pillars shift constantly up and down, while smaller boulders drift unpredictably around them. The chaotic motion makes every step hazardous, with a single misstep threatening a crushing blow or a deadly fall. Beneath the canyon’s evermoving stones lies the Great Beneath, home to Jagara and the Mighty Gyrocope. From afar, the canyon’s ceaseless motion seems mesmerizing, but few dare cross it without careful planning and sturdy equipment.

#### DREARY CANAL

The Dreary Canal is one of the largest waterways of New Thundera, fed by the melting glaciers of the distant Caverns of Cold. True to its name, the canal winds through grim, lifeless terrain, its waters dark and still. Despite its desolate appearance, the canal is a vital watersource for the ThunderCats and home to strange and dangerous creatures, including the elusive flying serpent known as Eezuka. ADVENTURE HOOK Churning Rocks is the only route to reach the Great Beneath from the surface, making it a crucial path for travelers and adventurers. In addition, Thundera refugees were last seen nearby, and the robotic villain Two-Time and his ship, DomeDown, have also been spotted in its vicinity. Hidden tunnels within the canyon also lead toward the New Onyx Pyramid, offering secret paths for those daring enough to navigate them. ADVENTURE HOOK When the five Moons of Plun-Darr align in harmony, New Thundera suffers a devastating drought. Rivers and lakes vanish, and the Dreary Canal dries to a barren bed. To restore life to the canal, the parched landscape must be crossed, desperate and dangerous creatures confronted, and the Caverns of Cold reached to melt the glaciers and refill the canal with water.

#### EASTERN SEA

#### IRON GLADES

The Eastern Sea is a vast expanse of water dotted with towering sea spires. These swirling columns of water and wind rise from the surface into the sky, forming powerful tornadoes that can lift and drag anything nearby. The Iron Glades is a metallic forest where towering pine trees have transformed into solid iron. Their branches crackle with electricity, scattering glowing sparks through the misty air. Even the pinecones are forged of charged metal, humming faintly with energy as they drop upon intruders. Once a lush evergreen woodland, the forest was twisted into its current state after the planet’s destruction and rebirth, its essence corrupted by the influence of the Moons of Plun-Darr. ADVENTURE HOOK Long ago, an evil frog-like creature known as Frog-Man arrived on Thundera after his home world began to dry. Possessing control over water, he sought to dominate the land, but the wise Jaga trapped him in an underground lake. Now, careless digging or ill-timed storms could awaken Frog-Man, unleashing a devastating flood across New Thundera. Only by discovering a way to channel the chaotic energy of the Eastern Sea’s spires could heroes hope to contain the waters and confront the ancient, watery menace.

#### GREAT BENEATH

The Great Beneath is a vast underground cave system that stretches for miles beneath New Thundera’s Churning Canyon. Its primary resident is Jagara, an ancient sorceress who serves as the sole guardian of the Mighty Gyroscope. Hidden from the surface world, this subterranean realm is little known, yet it is vital to the planet’s survival. The cavern feels otherworldly, with walls that echo every sound and a persistent, eerie stillness. Deep within its labyrinthine tunnels, the controls of the Mighty Gyroscope are kept. Any disturbance could send chaos rippling across New Thundera. Jagara dedicates herself to safeguarding this hidden realm, though few have dared to venture so far into the planet’s depths. ADVENTURE HOOK At the heart of the Great Beneath lies the Mighty Gyroscope, an immense machine that generates New Thundera’s gravity. A colossal spinning disc at its center keeps the planet stable, but even a minor malfunction could trigger massive earthquakes and sudden shifts in gravitational pull. If the Gyroscope fails, the planet’s gravity could falter, risking catastrophic destruction or even drifting the world into space. Jagara constantly monitors the apparatus, defending it from any threat and ensuring it operates without fail. She rarely leaves her post, knowing that a single mistake could shatter New Thundera. ADVENTURE HOOK According to the Book of Omens, the Iron Glades is one of the sacred ritual sites required to restore New Thundera to its former splendor and avert its second destruction from the spreading corruption of the Moons of Plun-Darr. The Ancient ThunderCat Flag from the Treasure of Thundera must be brought to the Iron Glades and raised precisely at the twenty-fourth hour of the ritual. Only then can New Thundera be saved.

#### JUNGLES OF DARKNESS

The Jungles of Darkness are a shadow-drenched region where light itself seems to vanish. Bathed in the eerie influence of Plun-Darr’s Third Moon, the jungle remains in perpetual night, mirroring the moon’s own black surface. Within its depths, ordinary sight is useless and travelers must rely on special equipment or magical means to navigate the oppressive gloom. Without them, one could easily lose their way among the choking undergrowth and tangled roots. Beneath the canopy, unseen predators prowl, their movements masked by the endless dark. ADVENTURE HOOK Though treacherous, the jungles are believed to conceal remnants of old Thundera such as forgotten ruins, untapped resources, and perhaps even fragments of the legendary Treasure of Thundera. Drawn by the promise of discovery, daring scouts and fortune-seekers continue to brave the lightless wilds, vanishing one after another into its murky heart.

#### LAND OF NO RETURN

The Land of No Return lies beyond a shimmering energy barrier, sealed away for generations. Long ago, the Thunderians banished the dark creature Amortus to this forsaken place, imprisoning him within a barren wasteland where no life can thrive. He dwells in a giant cave on the side of a grotesquely shaped mountain with a jagged maw as an entrance that can open and close. Within this cave are the petrified forms of Thunderians who dared to venture too close. One touch from Amortus drains all life and turns flesh to cold stone. ADVENTURE HOOK Trapped within the Land of No Return, Amortus has reached beyond his prison to seek aid from Mumm-Ra. Yet MummRa, ever cautious of rivals, hesitates to unleash another power-hungry evil upon the universe. He offers his help only if Amortus first proves his worth by destroying Mumm-Ra’s enemies. Amortus cannot escape the Land of No Return on his own, yet though trapped, he is far from powerless, and his reach extends beyond its borders. Through dark rituals, he projects an ethereal form onto Thundera, where he marks chosen victims with his touch. His tentacle leaves a faint sigil, allowing him to whisper into the victim’s mind and plant subtle compulsions. Those afflicted remember only strange dreams or an uneasy sense of dread, unaware that their thoughts no longer belong entirely to them.

#### MOSSLANDS

The Mosslands stretch across an uncharted corner of New Thundera, a wild expanse blanketed in thick, spongy moss and scattered with jagged rocky outcrops. Every step sinks slightly into the soft green carpet, and the air is heavy with the scent of damp earth and ancient growth. ADVENTURE HOOK As an uncharted land, the mosslands may conceal fragments of the Treasure of Thundera, along with dangers yet unknown. Indeed, this place is home to the massive mossland monster, a fiercely territorial creature that strikes anything it sees as a threat. Its camouflage is perfect, allowing it to blend seamlessly with the moss and ambush travelers, turning intruders into moss themselves.

#### MOUNTAINS OF THE MOON

The Mountains of the Moon loom over New Thundera with such intense gravity, that walking there feels like carrying a boulder on your back. Once an important signal tower, the range has become a punishing domain under the influence of Plun-Darr’s first moon, infamous for its crushing pull. Jagged cliffs, loose rocks, and dizzying heights turn every step into a trial. Without the proper gear, even setting foot here can be enough to collapse under the strain. ADVENTURE HOOK Despite the dangers, the ThunderCats see opportunity in the Mountains of the Moon. The peaks may hide rare resources or vantage points perfect for scouting enemies. Every expedition demands careful planning, precise timing, and nerves of steel. One misstep could leave a climber trapped, pinned under their own weight in a place few would ever find.

#### NEW CATS’ LAIR

New Cats’ Lair serves as the home and base of operations for the ThunderCats on New Thundera. After choosing to settle permanently on the re-formed planet, the ThunderCats built their new home in the Valley of the Stone Giants, following an encounter with the valley’s massive inhabitants. The lair is shaped like a colossal lion, larger and more advanced than the original on Third Earth. Construction was carried out by the snarfs, but progress was repeatedly delayed by violent earthquakes triggered when Mumm-Ra stole the regulator from the Mighty Gyroscope. On top of the tremors, gravitational anomalies in the valley frequently destroyed the snarfs’ work, making the construction a long and difficult endeavor. Ultimately, the ThunderCats defeated Mumm-Ra and rebuilt the gyroscope, completing their fortress. The result is a hightech stronghold with formidable defenses, ready to protect its inhabitants and serve as a center for ThunderCat operations. ADVENTURE HOOK Unlike the Cats’ Lair on Third Earth, New Cats’ Lair is rarely attacked head-on. Instead, threats usually come in the form of infiltration, sabotage, or subterfuge. The ThunderCats must be constantly vigilant for spies within the lair, magical tampering with its defenses, or mysterious disturbances linked to lingering anomalies in the valley.

#### NEW ONYX PYRAMID

The New Onyx Pyramid is the collective name for the three polyhedral structures that serve as Mumm-Ra’s home on New Thundera. When the planet first reformed, he quickly built a pyramid reminiscent of his old lair on Third Earth, featuring a central pyramid surrounded by four eerie obelisks. Inside, his skull-shaped sarcophagus was redesigned, and his magical cauldron took on an octagonal form instead of the usual circular shape.

During a confrontation with Lion-O, Mumm-Ra accidentally destroyed the pyramid. He hurled the Sword of Plun-Darr at Lion-O, missed, and struck one of the Ancient Spirits of Evil statues instead, causing the entire structure to collapse. Without a proper lair, Mumm-Ra conjured a temporary chamber in a hidden cave. He placed his sarcophagus, cauldron, and the four statues inside, creating a secret base from which he could continue monitoring the ThunderCats when not on Third Earth. Later on, the Ancient Spirits of Evil lifted the banishment they had imposed on Mumm-Ra and created a second New Onyx Pyramid for him on New Thundera. This new structure matches the original in design but features a more pockmarked exterior and is set amidst the ruins of Thundera’s former capital city, Grimalkingrad. ADVENTURE HOOK The New Onyx Pyramid is easier to assault than its Third Earth counterpart, as Mumm-Ra has far less control over the surrounding terrain. Reaching the pyramid, however, requires navigating the cracked streets and crumbling buildings of Grimalkingrad. While Mumm-Ra cannot command the ruins, all manner of hazards and dangers inhabit them, making the approach as perilous as the lair itself.

#### SWAMP OF THE LAUGHING LILY PADS

Among the many wetlands of New Thundera, few are as strange or as dangerous as the Swamp of the Laughing Lily Pads. Its shimmering waters appear harmless, yet anyone who drinks from them soon erupts into uncontrollable laughter. Without swift treatment, the laughter can turn fatal. The swamp’s surface is blanketed in great, floating lily pads that drift lazily over the treacherous waters. ADVENTURE HOOK The swamp’s magic keeps its waters pure and plentiful, even during droughts, making it a tempting resource for nearby settlements. However, the swamp is home to the laughing lily pads, plants that can crawl across the water’s surface and use their vine-like tendrils to seize the unwary. Those who are dragged below soon fall victim to the swamp’s deadly laughter.

#### THUNDERSTONE MOUNTAIN

Thunderstone Mountain is a rocky expanse of jagged peaks and splintered cliffs. What appears to be an ordinary mountain range is actually riddled with thunderstone, a rare mineral known for its explosive instability. Even the faintest disturbance can trigger devastating eruptions. ADVENTURE HOOK Deep within the mountain lie several thundrillium mines. Yet, due to the thunderstone, every strike of a pick risks disaster, as the slightest mistake can set off a chain reaction of explosions capable of collapsing tunnels and trapping miners within.

#### VALLEY OF THE SNARFS

Nestled among Thundera’s rolling lowlands, the Valley of the Snarfs was once a quiet haven where the snarfs lived simple, contented lives. Cozy homes dotted the winding paths, and every garden bloomed under their care. Life was peaceful, until the destruction of Thundera forced the snarfs to flee and seek refuge on the Planet of Snarfs. Yet peace did not last. When Mumm-Ra began his search for the fabled Treasure of Thundera, he kidnapped the snarfs from their new planet, enslaving them to dig through ruins across the planet. Only after the ThunderCats stormed his excavation sites were the snarfs freed and returned to their cherished valley. Miraculously, when Thundera was later reborn as New

Thundera, the valley endured. Much of it remained intact, a rare remnant of the world that was. There, they once again tended their homes and gardens, hoping that peace had finally taken root. ADVENTURE HOOK Mumm-Ra, ever scheming, has created a cursed artifact known as the Locket of Lies. He sent Ma-Mutt to secretly plant it in the Valley of the Snarfs, where it was soon discovered by the curious creatures. Believing it to be a lost relic of the Treasure of Thundera, they eagerly plan to contact the ThunderCats through their Snarf Communicator to share the news. The locket resembles an ancient compass, its needle fixed on a mysterious direction. Those who follow its pull are led deep into forgotten ruins, only to plunge into a sealed underground crypt. Each attempt to escape causes the chamber’s walls to close in, further sealing their fate. who drink from it are overcome with self-doubt, their confidence draining away until they can no longer trust their own judgment. The more one drinks, the deeper the despair grows. Guarding the Well are two towering stone statues which can be animated by Baron Tass through a magical amulet. ADVENTURE HOOK Baron Tass is a cunning and unscrupulous noble who escaped Thundera’s destruction with his wealth intact. In the old world, he thrived on deception, trapping others in endless debts through exploitative bargains. Now, in New Thundera, the ThunderCats strive to rebuild society as a place of equality and fresh beginnings, free from the chains of the past. Outraged by this new order, Baron Tass plots to reclaim his influence by spreading water from the Well of Doubt among the ThunderCats, hoping to fill them with uncertainty and reclaim power while their resolve falters.

#### VALLEY OF THE STONE GIANTS

The Valley of the Stone Giants is a rugged region on New Thundera filled with colossal stone figures that resemble ancient Thunderian warriors, their heads and shoulders rising above the earth like silent sentinels. These are no ordinary statues, they are living guardians that awaken whenever danger draws near. When the ThunderCats returned to New Thundera to begin resettlement, they landed in this valley. Their arrival stirred the stone giants, who mistook them for intruders. Lion-O quickly intervened, assuring the giants that the ThunderCats came in peace. Once convinced, the giants returned to stillness, and the valley became the perfect site for the construction of the New Cats’ Lair. Ever since, the giants have remained nearby in slumbering form, vigilant protectors against any incoming threat. ADVENTURE HOOK When a great threat endangers the ThunderCats, even beyond the valley, the Sword of Omens can summon the power of the stone giants. This power may awaken a giant who will then march to their aid. In moments of dire peril, the Sword of Omens can even be placed in the hands of a stone giant itself, turning the ancient guardian into a towering champion of Thundera.

#### WELL OF DOUBT

Hidden deep within a cave on the former estate of Baron Tass lies the Well of Doubt. At first glance, it appears to be a simple underground water source, but its waters are cursed. Those

### Planets of Interest

While most ThunderCats action unfolds on Third Earth and New Thundera, their adventures have reached far beyond those worlds. Several other planets have become entangled with their journey, each playing its own part in the ThunderCats’ story. Though countless worlds exist across the cosmos, the following are those most closely tied to their saga.

#### BLACK PLANET

The Black Planet is a vast celestial body that drifts near the Star of Thundera, an astral entity unlike any other. Where most stars blaze with burning gas, the Star of Thundera appears as a glowing red world, alive and aware, brimming with unfathomable power. The nearby Black Planet exerts a gravitational pull so immense that even ethereal beings are drawn toward its depths. Once caught in its field, nothing living, dead, or spectral can escape. ADVENTURE HOOK Legends say the Star of Thundera can grant aid to those it deems worthy, even capable of taking the form of a talisman that nullifies the withering effects of thundrainium. However, reaching the Star means venturing perilously close to the Black Planet’s crushing pull. Those seeking the Star’s aid must find a way to resist the Black Planet’s gravity or risk being lost forever to the void.

#### BLUE PLUNDER

Blue Plunder is a distant azure world, located a thousand light-years from Third Earth. Little is known about the planet beyond its striking coloration and the mineral that powers its civilization, a common blue-hued rock found by the billions beneath planet oceans. ADVENTURE HOOK As Blue Plunder’s own reserves dwindled, its leaders dispatched teams of salvage experts to harvest the mineral from other worlds. These crews show no interest in diplomacy or fair trade, seizing what they need with ruthless efficiency. Entire seas may be stripped bare as they work to keep their homeworld alive, no matter the cost to others.

#### EXILE ISLE

Exile Isle is an asteroid-sized planetoid drifting in the far reaches of space. Because of its small size and remote location, many refer to it simply as an “island.” Long ago, it was home to salvage pirates known as shipwreckers. Using massive giant bellows of their own design, they stirred up ash from a dormant volcano and shrouded the entire world in a dense haze. This fog, which they called the Devil’s Fog, concealed the planet from approaching vessels who would receive their transmitted distress call, causing ships to crash upon its surface. The shipwreckers would then scavenge the wrecks for parts, treasure, and prisoners. ADVENTURE HOOK The ThunderCats have constructed a one-way teleporter to Exile Isle, intending to use it as a prison for their enemies. Unfortunately, the island is not as deserted as they believe. Some of the old shipwreckers still linger, maintaining the ancient mechanisms that once lured travelers to their doom. The ThunderCats’ new teleportation device may accidentally reactivate the shipwreckers, trapping a fresh group of desperate souls who will do anything to find their way back to Third Earth.

#### GRAY PENAL PLANET

The Gray Penal Planet, sometimes called the Gray Prison Planet, is a massive, hemispherical world that functions as a galactic super-prison. It resembles a space station more than a natural planet, with most of it forged from metal and reinforced with powerful containment fields. Controlled by the Interplanetary Control Force, it serves as the final destination for the galaxy’s most dangerous and irredeemable offenders. Access is heavily restricted, and the ICF maintains strict patrols to ensure no prisoner escapes and no outsider enters without authorization.

#### NEPTUNE

Navy Engineers Power Tower Under Nuclear Energy, or NEPTUNE, is an ancient artificial intelligence supercomputer built to maintain safe passage through space. Millennia ago, the Venutian Public Works of Venus constructed the immense power station to clear the space-ways of derelicts, failed satellites, and other hazardous debris. It operated within an artificial black hole, powered by nuclear energy and stabilized by a massive gravitational field. When Venus burned out thousands of years ago, NEPTUNE was abandoned, left with only its AI to ADVENTURE HOOK Among the countless stars drift those who live outside the law, and many have found themselves imprisoned upon the Gray Penal Planet. With so many high-profile captives gathered in one place, whispers of jailbreaks are inevitable. While the ICF’s security is formidable, cunning smugglers and rebel crews have begun uncovering weaknesses in the outer defenses. Most would dream of escaping such a place, not infiltrating it. Should any breach occur, the ICF stands ready to call upon the ThunderCats for aid before chaos consumes the prison world. oversee it. The surrounding region now forms a vast graveyard of ships and satellites, orbiting endlessly in the darkness. Within its core chamber, NEPTUNE’s intelligence manifests as a levitating metallic sphere, though its consciousness extends throughout the entire power station. Every wall, console, and corridor hums with its sentient presence. Over time, the AI’s programming has grown unstable, and now claims all property entering the station, living or not, by salvage right.

ADVENTURE HOOK Mistaking NEPTUNE’s false black hole for the real thing, Mumm-Ra intends to cast the Sword of Omens into its depths forever. To lure the ThunderCats into the trap, he’s enlisted Captain Shiner of the starship Vertus, hiring the mercenary to broadcast a distress signal from near the anomaly. Once the ThunderCats arrive, Mumm-Ra will ambush them, seize the sword, and hurl it into the “black hole.” However, the scheme is particularly risky for Shiner. His ship risks being caught in NEPTUNE’s magnetic field, or worse, dragged in if the ThunderCats retaliate. Should any vessel cross the event horizon, it will be pulled inside the NEPTUNE complex, where the AI enforces its salvage laws. All who enter are declared property of the station and confined within its titanium rest chambers, once used to house weary spacefarers. To escape, the captives must uncover NEPTUNE’s controls and reverse the magnetic polarity, causing the simulated black hole to violently eject everything trapped within it. This desperate maneuver unleashes a maelstrom of debris and energy, scattering wreckage across the stars.

#### ONYX

Onyx is a shadowed world shrouded in perpetual gloom, its surface dominated by vast stretches of gassy swamplands. Thick mists cling to every bog and marsh, concealing the movements of creatures and civilizations that thrive beneath the darkness. Though advanced societies are rumored to dwell deep within these swamps, few outsiders have ventured far enough to confirm the truth. ADVENTURE HOOK The infamous criminal Plutar hails from this bleak planet. Known throughout the galaxy for his toxic touch, Plutar’s very hands exude a lethal poison that withers all living things. Trees rot, flowers blacken, and animals perish wherever he passes. His only goal is to spread death and decay across every world he encounters. Entire planets have fallen to ruin because of his deadly gift, and the Interplanetary Control Force has placed the highest possible bounty on his capture.

#### PLANET OF SNARFS

The Planet of Snarfs is a vibrant world that became the new home of the Snarf species after the fall of Thundera. Lush, colorful, and brimming with life, nearly anything planted in its fertile soil will grow, making it a paradise for the gentle and resourceful snarfs. When Thundera teetered on the brink of destruction, forty-three snarfs managed to seize an old mu’tant tanker and escape the planet’s final explosion. Drifting through the stars, they discovered an uninhabited world rich in natural beauty and abundant resources. They made it their own, naming it simply the Planet of Snarfs. ADVENTURE HOOK Peace on the Planet of Snarfs did not last. Word of the snarfs’ survival soon reached the mu’tants and Mumm-Ra, who saw an opportunity to strike at the ThunderCats through their smallest allies. Now, the snarfs live in constant fear of raids and kidnappings, and they desperately need brave defenders to help protect their idyllic world.

father-in-law-to-be, is cursed to come alive and devour the samurai before returning to its miniature form. Jaga later rescued the doomed samurai and secured the statue within the Treasure of Thundera. When the Sword of Omens shattered the chest, the dragon statue was lost somewhere on New Thundera. Mumm-Ra knows the statue’s true power and lies in wait for the day it is rediscovered, eager to unleash it upon his enemies. Only Hachiman knows how to lift the curse, able to cleave the statue in two with his enchanted blade, but Mumm-Ra has already set plans in motion to stop him should he try.

#### PLUN-DARR

#### PLANET OF THE RED SUN

Home to the Samurai Empire, the Planet of the Red Sun is an ancient colony of humans devoted to the code of Bushido. This code emphasizes loyalty, honor, and courage above all, supported by virtues such as rectitude, benevolence, politeness, sincerity, and self-control. The Samurai Empire thrives in a culture that blends tradition with technology. Its cities appear rooted in a bygone era, with ornate temples and wooden homes, yet beneath this rustic beauty lies a world of advanced machinery. Magic also flows through their society, and enchanted weapons and armor are commonplace among the Empire’s proud and disciplined warriors. Orbiting opposite Thundera lies its sister planet, Plun-Darr. Once a sun-dappled paradise rivaled in beauty only by Thundera, it has become a dark and blasted world. Centuries of internal conflict among the mu’tants, combined with the corrupting influence of its moons, have twisted the planet into a world of boiling oceans, dreary swamps, and scum-covered deserts. The mu’tants are a fierce warrior creed, locked in endless conflict with one another and the wider galaxy since the invention of the spiked club. Their oldest foes are the ThunderCats, whose planet they have repeatedly tried to conquer after stealing the secret of space travel from a visiting star-galleon which was made to crash as soon as it entered Plun-Farran airspace. Debris from Thundera’s destruction has further devastated Plun-Darr, sending its moons drifting from orbit. Many of its inhabitants perished in the initial chaos, and now those that survived are fractured, their warlike culture struggling to cooperate as leadership crumbles under the strain of loss and isolation. ADVENTURE HOOK Prince Hachiman, the fiery-hearted champion of the Samurai Empire, has been both ally and adversary to the ThunderCats. His short temper and overconfidence often cloud his judgment, traits that Mumm-Ra has repeatedly exploited. Despite this, Hachiman remains a deeply honorable warrior, ever ready to risk his life to aid the ThunderCats against dire threats, such as that of the cursed Double Headed Dragon of Doom, one of the fabled pieces of the Treasure of Thundera. This tiny statue, once bewitched by a samurai’s resentful

ADVENTURE HOOK Currently, the mu’tants on Third Earth are the only Plun-Darran threat to the ThunderCats, as the rest of their homeworld struggles to recover from Thundera’s destruction. Each month, however, Plun-Darr’s restoration progresses, and the Third Earth mu’tants have begun communicating with warlords still on Plun-Darr, seeking reinforcements. One day, those messages may bring new dangers to the ThunderCats.

#### ASTRAL WORLD

#### RO-BEAR

ADVENTURE HOOK A powerful and malevolent presence in the Astral World is Nemex, a one-eyed being who serves as the principal warden of an astral prison. Nemex traps ghosts and exiled mortal beings within his impenetrable fortress. Surrounding the prison is a moat inhabited by a fearsome Astral Moat Monster. Those who become trapped in the Astral World, or are bound to its shifting expanse, may be confronted by Nemex. Escape is rare, for travel to the Astral World is often one-way. Only the wizard Brodo, imprisoned by Nemex, holds the secret to returning visitors to the mortal plane. Ro-Bear is a small, tranquil planet inhabited by the robotic bear-like berbils. The planet is dotted with settlements surrounded by fields of berbil fruit. For generations, the gentle and peace-loving berbils lived happily on Ro-Bear. As their population grew, however, the planet became increasingly crowded. To find new homes, groups of berbils have begun departing in spaceships, venturing out into the stars. ADVENTURE HOOK With countless berbil pilgrims journeying among the stars in search of new homes, encounters with these travelers are possible for any passing starship. Such meetings can be fortuitous for those in need, as berbils are almost always eager to help. However, these encounters can become dangerous for the berbils if a starship’s crew proves less than friendly.

#### WAY OUTBACK

Way Outback is a small, star-shaped planetoid often described as an “island” floating in space. Captain Bragg, a notorious intergalactic bounty hunter, uses it as a prison to contain the most dangerous criminals. This remote location ensures that troublemakers are kept far from inhabited planets and the wider galaxy. ADVENTURE HOOK Captain Bragg travels the cosmos aboard his Circus Train, offering his services to rid planets of undesirable characters for a price. Dressed in a flamboyant ringmaster-style outfit, he is both a master showman and a cunning con artist, using clever tricks and schemes to achieve his goals. While Bragg keeps Way Outback under strict control, no prison is perfect. Occasionally, a prisoner escapes. When that happens, Bragg may call upon the ThunderCats to track down and recapture the fugitive.

### Other Dimensions

ThunderCats adventures extend far beyond the boundaries of planets, reaching even into other dimensions and planes of existence. While countless realms may lie beyond what is known, the ones described here are those that have most closely intersected with the ThunderCats’ journeys. The Astral World exists beyond the mortal realm. Here, the usual laws of nature, space, and time no longer apply. The entire plane shimmers as if made of crystal, and its strange, often dangerous inhabitants drift through the endless expanse. It is said to be the dwelling place of ghosts, including legendary figures such as Jaga the Wise and Grune the Destroyer.

#### SEVENTH DIMENSION

The Seventh Dimension is one of many alternate realities that overlap the mortal realm. Little is known of its nature, for those who find themselves trapped within can return only for one brief day every seven thousand years, when the Seventh Dimension aligns with the mortal plane. During that fleeting moment, those ensnared within may cross over, but if they linger beyond the day’s end, their forms wither and fade into death. Each world has its own point of contact with the Seventh Dimension, a place where the veil between realities grows thin. ADVENTURE HOOK The Seventh Dimension serves as the prison of Wizz-Ra, the ancient Egyptian sorcerer who once waged war against Mumm-Ra’s corruption. Wizz-Ra’s Golden Helmet of PharNoor, a relic granting dominion over the minds of others, became the object of Mumm-Ra’s obsession. Ages ago, the two clashed in the skies above the ancient Sphinx. When Mumm-Ra struck a decisive blow, Wizz-Ra’s vessel plummeted into the Sphinx itself. Outraged that its sacred form had been defiled, the Sphinx unleashed its fury upon the fallen sorcerer, casting him into the Seventh Dimension for eternity. Now, the cycle turns once more. The single day of alignment draws near, and the Seventh Dimension will soon touch Third Earth again. In a fateful coincidence, its portal manifests upon the very mirror in the ThunderCat Cheetara’s chamber. Mumm-Ra intends to ambush Wizz-Ra the moment he emerges, seizing the Golden Helmet to restore his dominion before Wizz-Ra is forced to return to his prison for another seven millennia.

#### SHADOW REALM

The Shadow Realm lies far across the mystic void, a barren land of jagged dark flint rock. Its sky is a deep, featureless black, with only a massive glowing moon casting a silvery silhouette across the desolate terrain. Entry and exit are impossible through ordinary means, though small connections to the mortal plane can appear during eclipses or in places steeped in shadowy power, such as the Dark Moon of Plun-Darr. ADVENTURE HOOK Long ago, the maniacal Thunderian wizard known as the Shadowmaster was banished to the Shadow Realm for his evil deeds. Claudus, then Lord of the ThunderCats, captured him using the Sword of Omens and, with Jaga’s aid, sent him to this dark exile. For years, the Shadowmaster honed his powers within the rocky wasteland, constructing a fortress and observing the mortal plane. When Thundera teetered on the brink of destruction, he used his magic to capture Claudus, taking advantage of an eclipse to open a small portal into the Shadow Realm. Now, years later, an eclipse of the Dark Moon of Plun-Darr on new Thundera has allowed the Shadowmaster to contact the Ancient Spirits of Evil. He has struck a dangerous bargain in which they will free him from the Shadow Realm if he succeeds in destroying the ThunderCats. Using Claudus as bait, the Shadowmaster plans to lure the heroes into his dark domain, where deadly conjured shadow creatures and powerful magic await.

#### TIME WARP PRISON

The Time Warp Prison is an empty, disorienting expanse where nothing exists but dark, roiling skies and churning storm clouds. There is no ground, no horizon, and no familiar sense of direction. Within this dimension, time itself ceases to move. Days never pass, and creatures remain suspended, unaging and unchanged. This timeless dimension serves as an eternal prison. Prisoners are sent here through ritual circles of exile, often aboard guarded barges that drift endlessly through the void, ensuring that escape is impossible and their torment everlasting. ADVENTURE HOOK The ancient Mayan sorceress Ta-She was imprisoned in the Time Warp Prison long ago, punished for her malevolent deeds. Trapped with no hope of escape, she drifts endlessly on her spectral barge, accompanied only by alligator-like jailors. Legends say the Ancient Spirits of Evil granted Ta-She the enthralling power of the Doomgaze, and now they yearn for her return to the mortal plane. To fulfill their will, they have commanded Mumm-Ra to release her. To do so, he must collect the four items that together form the key to the Time Warp Prison: a tuft of a cheetah’s hair, a berbil’s tear, a unicorn’s shoe, and a hero to take Ta-She’s place. Only then can he perform the ritual atop Mount Anguish on Third Earth. Once she’s freed, the two vassals of the Ancient Spirits of Evil will combine their powers to cast a shadow over all of Third Earth.

## Chapter 3: Equipment and Vehicles

“Thundera had an abundance of resources. Silver, platinum and the most important resource that kept Thundera running,

### Thundrillium

our world. We were blessed. And the weapons of Thundera are the most feared and sought after by enemies and allies alike. Forged by the greatest, used by the most capable. There are none else like them.” —Cheetara the Fast The following are additional options for armor, weapons, adventuring gear, and magic items within ThunderCats adventures. This chapter also has an in-depth look at advanced thundrillium-based technology, including their usage and

### Availability

may be transferable to other campaign settings. Thundrillium Thundrillium is the precious substance that powers advanced technologies in ThunderCats adventures. While traditional electronic devices do still exist, the resources to build and maintain them on Third Earth and New Thundera are scarce. As a result, thundrillium remains the only reliable power source for most high-tech equipment. Thundrillium resembles gold in appearance, but it is far denser and has a subtly crystalline structure. Once mined from its deposits, the ore is melted down in specially designed furnaces. It is first purified of surface impurities, then smelted again to remove trace elements such as gold. The resulting pure, liquid thundrillium is either pumped directly into connected technology, such as the systems of Cats’ Lair or Sky-Tomb, or stored in energy cells for portable use. Availability The availability of thundrillium depends heavily on the planet and its level of development. Third Earth contains many deposits, but locating, mining, and refining them requires advanced technology, time, and significant risk. Once proper infrastructure is established, thundrillium becomes far easier to obtain. For guidance on how plentiful it can be, see the breakdown in Chapter 1, “Eras of Adventure”. If you as a GM are nervous about including too much of this technology in your game, you can further limit thundrillium’s presence. Most ThunderCats adventures already feature scarcity of resources, with the ThunderCats reserving nearly all available thundrillium to power Cats’ Lair, vehicles, and defensive systems. High-tech handheld weaponry is therefore rare. Even the ThunderCats’ enemies face similar shortages, leading both sides to rely more on melee weapons and magical enhancements than on advanced armaments. When thundrillium-powered small arms do appear, it is often during desperate conflicts. Such battles are dangerous, as depleting energy cells may leave vehicles stranded or fortresses unprotected. The ThunderCats typically avoid this kind of escalation, prioritizing survival and defense over reckless offense. Consequently, swords, staves, whips, and other low-tech weapons remain the norm. These weapons are often magically enchanted to make up for this, though sometimes have additional features when an energy cell is placed inside.

### Strongholds

Technological bastions such as Cats’ Lair, the Tower of Omens, Castle Plun-Darr, and Sky-Tomb rely on thundrillium to sustain their advanced systems. Each stronghold consumes one energy cell’s worth of power per day of operation. The GM may also determine that individual mechanisms within a stronghold, such as laser cannons, retractable bridges, or automated defenses, require additional charges from energy cells to function.

### Energy Cells

lies a vast power core where thundrillium is refined, purified, and distributed through smelting furnaces and conduits. This network channels vital energy to every system of the stronghold, keeping the lair active and ready for defense at all times.

### Thundrillium Devices

This section introduces thundrillium-powered technological devices and weapons. See Fifth Edition gaming rules for more information on explosives and other futuristic weapons which would count as Thundrillium Devices for the purposes of ThunderCats. To most inhabitants of Third Earth, Thundrillium Devices are mysteries of lost science. Unless a character is of a species or culture familiar with such technology (for example, a Thunderian or a mu’tant), these objects are strange and confusing relics. When a character attempts to understand or operate a Thundrillium Device, they must succeed on a DC 15 Intelligence check. Failing by 5 or more expends 1 charge from the device if it has an energy cell installed. A character who has previously seen a similar device in use, or operated one before, makes this check with Advantage. Because they are rare and useless without charged energy cells, Thundrillium Devices are considered priceless artifacts rather than tradeable goods. Thundrillium Devices are clearly referred to as such in the below list of various equipment. Energy Cells Thundrillium Devices draw energy from these Energy Cells. Each cell gives a number of charges depending on the device. Using the device expends a number of charges dependant on the device. Replacing Energy Cells in Thundrillium Devices. While a Thundrillium Device has charges remaining, its Energy Cell can’t be removed. Once the device has 0 charges, the Energy Cell can be replaced with a new cell by taking an action or a Bonus Action.

### Thundrainium

While thundrillium is an extraordinary power source, it cannot be directly used as weapons or armor. Thundrainium, on the other hand, can. This equally remarkable substance emits radiation harmful to Thunderians, which is why it is never used by the ThunderCats and is typically wielded only by their enemies. Thundrainium can be melted and applied as a coating to weapons and armor, granting a detrimental effect against Thunderians. However, this coating is fragile and it can be worn away through scouring or deliberate chipping. Despite these limitations, enemies of the ThunderCats consider the investment well worth it. Thunderian Withering. All thundrainium gives the Pacified condition to Thunderians within 20 feet of it, and additionally the Weakened condition to Thunderians within 10 feet of it. The Pacified and Weakened conditions caused by thundrainium end when a Thunderian moves at least 25 feet away from the source of thundrainium.

#### WEAPONS

WEAPONS Weapons coated in thundrainium lose its effect after hitting a target 10 times. Weapons coated in thundrainium have the following additional property. Thundera’s Bane. You have Advantage on attack rolls made with this weapon against Thunderians, and when you score a Critical Hit with it against a Thunderian, the weapon deals one additional die of damage.
